   481	
   482	    fun handleSystemBack(): Boolean {
   483	        if (activeMockFlow == null || mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress <= 0f) return false
   484	        closeMockSignInScreen()
   485	        return true
   486	    }
   487	
   488	    fun restoreIntroSnapshot(snapshot: CinerificIntroSnapshot) {
   489	        if (appliedIntroSnapshot == snapshot) return
   490	
   491	        val restoredFlow = enumValueOrNull<MockAccountFlow>(snapshot.activeFlowName)
   492	        val restoredRecoveryTarget = enumValueOrNull<MockForgotRecoveryTarget>(
   493	            snapshot.forgotRecoveryTargetName
   494	        ) ?: MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password
   495	        val restoredSubmissionState = enumValueOrNull<MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState>(
   496	            snapshot.forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName
   497	        ) ?: MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Idle
   498	
   499	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   500	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   501	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   502	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   503	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   504	        pressedMockField = null
   505	        pressedRememberMe = false
   506	        pressedBackChevron = false
   507	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   508	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   509	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   510	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   511	        lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   512	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   513	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   514	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   515	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   516	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   517	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   518	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   519	        activeComposingText = ""
   520	        expandedMockDropdown = null
   521	        focusedMockField = null
   522	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
   523	        mockInputDimActiveField = null
   524	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
   525	
   526	        activeMockFlow = restoredFlow
   527	        if (restoredFlow == null) {
   528	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
   529	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
   530	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
   531	        } else {
   532	            mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   533	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
   534	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
   535	        }
   536	
   537	        rememberMeChecked = snapshot.rememberMeChecked
   538	        usernameText = snapshot.usernameText
   539	        passwordText = snapshot.passwordText
   540	        confirmPasswordText = snapshot.confirmPasswordText
   541	        emailText = snapshot.emailText
   542	        monthText = snapshot.monthText
   543	        dayText = snapshot.dayText
   544	        yearText = snapshot.yearText
   545	        subscriptionTierText = normalizedMembershipTierSelection(snapshot.subscriptionTierText)
   546	        forgotRecoveryTarget = restoredRecoveryTarget
   547	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = restoredSubmissionState
   548	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = if (
   549	            restoredSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading
   550	        ) {
   551	            SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   552	        } else {
   553	            null
   554	        }
   555	
   556	        mockCreateAvatarIndex = snapshot.createAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
   557	        portraitProfileIndex = snapshot.portraitProfileIndex.coerceIn(0, FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.lastIndex)
   558	        resetPortraitProfileCarousel()
   559	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis = null
   560	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
   561	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
   562	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection = MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next
   563	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartProgress = 0f
   564	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets.keys.forEach { dropdown ->
   565	            mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = 0
   566	        }
   567	
   568	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
   569	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
   570	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   571	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   572	        appliedIntroSnapshot = currentIntroSnapshot()
   573	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   574	    }
   575	
   576	    override fun onSizeChanged(width: Int, height: Int, oldWidth: Int, oldHeight: Int) {
   577	        super.onSizeChanged(width, height, oldWidth, oldHeight)
   578	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   579	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   580	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   581	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   582	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   583	        pressedMockField = null
   584	        pressedRememberMe = false
   585	        pressedBackChevron = false
   586	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   587	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   588	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   589	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   590	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   591	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   592	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   593	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   594	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   595	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   596	        resetPortraitProfileCarousel()
   597	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   598	    }
   599	
   600	    override fun onApplyWindowInsets(insets: WindowInsets): WindowInsets {
   601	        val systemBars = WindowInsetsCompat.toWindowInsetsCompat(insets, this)
   602	            .getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.systemBars())
   603	        systemBarInsetTopPx = systemBars.top
   604	        systemBarInsetBottomPx = systemBars.bottom
   605	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   606	        return super.onApplyWindowInsets(insets)
   607	    }
   608	
   609	    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
   610	        super.onDraw(canvas)
   611	
   612	        if (IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis == 0L) {
   613	            IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   614	        }
   615	        val progress = bootProgressAt(IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis)
   616	        val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
   617	            progress,
   618	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
   619	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
   620	        )
   621	        val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2500, 3900)
   622	        drawIntroBackground(canvas, blackToPurple, purpleToSettle)
   623	
   624	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return
   625	        finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded()
   626	        finishMockFlowSwitchIfNeeded()
   627	        finishPortraitProfileCarouselIfNeeded()
   628	        finishMockCreateAvatarCarouselIfNeeded()
   629	        finishMockForgotPasswordSubmissionIfNeeded()
   630	        val mockProgress = mockSignInProgress()
   631	        updateMockFormFocusAnimation()
   632	        updateMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   633	        updateMockInputDimAnimation()
   634	        val formFocusMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress)
   635	
   636	        val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(progress, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   637	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   638	            drawSettlingLogo(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, progress, logoAlpha)
   639	        }
   640	
   641	        val avatarAlpha = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   642	        val mockMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockProgress)
   643	        val createWelcomeTransition = isMockCreateWelcomeTransition()
   644	        if (avatarAlpha > 0.01f) {
   645	            val stackAlpha = avatarAlpha * (1f - mockMotion)
   646	            val y = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha) + MOCK_SIGN_IN_STACK_EXIT_Y * mockMotion
   647	            if (!createWelcomeTransition && stackAlpha > 0.01f) {
   648	                drawWelcomeAvatarStack(canvas, stage, y, stackAlpha)
   649	            }
   650	        }
   651	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   652	            val outgoingFlow = outgoingMockFlow
   653	            if (outgoingFlow != null) {
   654	                val switchMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFlowSwitchProgress())
   655	                drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
   656	                    canvas,
   657	                    outgoingFlow,
   658	                    stage.left,
   659	                    stage.top,
   660	                    stage.scale,
   661	                    1f - switchMotion,
   662	                    formFocusMotion,
   663	                    updateLabelAnimation = false
   664	                )
   665	                activeMockFlow?.let { incomingFlow ->
   666	                    drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
   667	                        canvas,
   668	                        incomingFlow,
   669	                        stage.left,
   670	                        stage.top,
   671	                        stage.scale,
   672	                        switchMotion,
   673	                        formFocusMotion,
   674	                        updateLabelAnimation = true
   675	                    )
   676	                }
   677	            } else {
   678	                drawMockSignInForm(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, mockMotion, formFocusMotion)
   679	            }
   680	            drawBackChevron(
   681	                canvas,
   682	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X,
   683	                mockBackChevronRowTopY(),
   684	                stage.left,
   685	                if (usesPortraitStackedAuthLayout()) {
   686	                    stage.top
   687	                } else {
   688	                    accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale)
   689	                },
   690	                stage.scale,
   691	                mockMotion
   692	            )
   693	        }
   694	        drawAccountPrompt(
   695	            canvas = canvas,
   696	            stageLeft = stage.left,
   697	            stageTop = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) stage.top else accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale),
   698	            stageScale = stage.scale,
   699	            yOffset = if (mockSignInStartMillis == null) {
   700	                lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha)
   701	            } else {
   702	                0f
   703	            },
   704	            alpha = avatarAlpha
   705	        )
   706	        drawMockCreateAvatarTransitionOverlay(
   707	            canvas,
   708	            stage,
   709	            mockMotion,
   710	            formFocusMotion
   711	        )
   712	
   713	        if (
   714	            progress < 1f ||
   715	            isMockSignInAnimating() ||
   716	            isMockFlowSwitchAnimating() ||
   717	            isMockFormLabelAnimating() ||
   718	            isMockFormFocusAnimating() ||
   719	            isMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimating() ||
   720	            isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating() ||
   721	            isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating() ||
   722	            isMockForgotPasswordSubmissionAnimating() ||
   723	            isMockInputDimAnimating()
   724	        ) {
   725	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   726	        }
   727	    }
   728	
   729	    override fun onTouchEvent(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
   730	        return when (event.actionMasked) {
   731	            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
   732	                mockTouchDownX = event.x
   733	                mockTouchDownY = event.y
   734	                portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
   735	                portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
   736	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   737	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   738	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   739	                    portraitAuthScrollStartY = event.y
   740	                    portraitAuthScrollStartOffset = portraitAuthScrollY
   741	                    portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
   742	                    if (isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) {
   743	                        return true
   744	                    }
   745	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   746	                    pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   747	                        settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   748	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   749	                        !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   750	                        settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   751	                    if (
   752	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   753	                        pressedSignInPrompt ||
   754	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt
   755	                    ) {
   756	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   757	                        return true
   758	                    }
   759	                    val touchedDropdownOptions = mockDropdownOptionsHit(event.x, event.y)
   760	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = touchedDropdownOptions?.takeIf { mockDropdownCanScroll(it) }
   761	                    mockDropdownScrollStartY = event.y
   762	                    mockDropdownScrollStartOffset = activeMockDropdownScroll?.let { mockDropdownScrollOffset(it) } ?: 0
   763	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = if (touchedDropdownOptions != null) {
   764	                        mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   765	                    } else {
   766	                        null
   767	                    }
   768	                    pressedMockDropdown = if (pressedMockDropdownOption == null && touchedDropdownOptions == null) {
   769	                        mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   770	                    } else {
   771	                        null
   772	                    }
   773	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = if (
   774	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   775	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   776	                        pressedMockDropdown == null
   777	                    ) {
   778	                        mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   779	                    } else {
   780	                        null
   781	                    }
   782	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   783	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   784	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   785	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   786	                        mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   787	                    val touchedCreateAvatarCarousel = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   788	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   789	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   790	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   791	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   792	                        mockCreateAvatarCarouselHit(event.x, event.y) &&
   793	                        !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
   794	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   795	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   796	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   797	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   798	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = if (
   799	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   800	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   801	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   802	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   803	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   804	                        touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   805	                    ) {
   806	                        mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   807	                    } else {
   808	                        null
   809	                    }
   810	                    pressedMockField = if (
   811	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   812	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   813	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   814	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   815	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   816	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   817	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   818	                    ) {
   819	                        mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   820	                    } else {
   821	                        null
   822	                    }
   823	                    pressedRememberMe = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   824	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   825	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   826	                        pressedMockField == null &&
   827	                        mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   828	                    pressedBackChevron = pressedMockField == null &&
   829	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   830	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   831	                        !pressedRememberMe &&
   832	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   833	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   834	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   835	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   836	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   837	                        mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   838	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   839	                    return true
   840	                }
   841	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   842	                pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   843	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   844	                    !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   845	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   846	                pressedAvatarProfile = if (
   847	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   848	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   849	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   850	                    isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()
   851	                ) {
   852	                    null
   853	                } else {
   854	                    settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   855	                }
   856	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   857	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   858	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   859	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null
   860	            }
   861	            MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
   862	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   863	                    if (updatePortraitAuthScroll(event)) {
   864	                        return true
   865	                    }
   866	                    if (updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event)) {
   867	                        return true
   868	                    }
   869	                    if (updateMockDropdownScroll(event)) {
   870	                        return true
   871	                    }
   872	                    if (mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) == null && shouldStartMockDragReturn(event)) {
   873	                        mockDragReturnInProgress = true
   874	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
   875	                    }
   876	                    return true
   877	                }
   878	                if (isPortraitIntroLayout() && pressedAvatarProfile != null) {
   879	                    val horizontalDelta = event.x - mockTouchDownX
   880	                    val verticalDelta = event.y - mockTouchDownY
   881	                    if (abs(horizontalDelta) > touchSlop && abs(horizontalDelta) > abs(verticalDelta)) {
   882	                        portraitProfileSwipeMoved = true
   883	                    }
   884	                    if (portraitProfileSwipeMoved) {
   885	                        portraitProfileDragDeltaX = horizontalDelta
   886	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   887	                    }
   888	                    return true
   889	                }
   890	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   891	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   892	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   893	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null
   894	            }
   895	            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
   896	                if (portraitAuthScrollMoved) {
   897	                    portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
   898	                    pressedMockField = null
   899	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   900	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   901	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   902	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   903	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   904	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   905	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   906	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   907	                    return true
   908	                }
   909	                if (mockDragReturnInProgress) {
   910	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   911	                    pressedMockField = null
   912	                    pressedRememberMe = false
   913	                    pressedBackChevron = false
   914	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   915	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   916	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   917	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   918	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   919	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   920	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   921	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   922	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   923	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   924	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   925	                    return true
   926	                }
   927	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   928	                    if (isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) {
   929	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   930	                        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   931	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   932	                        return true
   933	                    }
   934	                    val requestedFlow = when {
   935	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt && settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   936	                            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
   937	                        }
   938	                        pressedSignInPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   939	                            MockAccountFlow.SignIn
   940	                        }
   941	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt && settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   942	                            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
   943	                        }
   944	                        else -> null
   945	                    }
   946	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   947	                    pressedSignInPrompt = false
   948	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   949	                    if (requestedFlow != null) {
   950	                        super.performClick()
   951	                        switchMockAccountFlow(requestedFlow)
   952	                        return true
   953	                    }
   954	                    val releasedDropdownOption = mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   955	                    val releasedDropdown = mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   956	                    val releasedField = mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   957	                    val releasedRememberMe = mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   958	                    val releasedBackChevron = mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   959	                    val releasedCreateAvatarNav = mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   960	                    val releasedForgotRecoveryTarget = mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   961	                    val releasedForgotPasswordSubmit = mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   962	                    val wasTouchingDropdownOptions = activeMockDropdownScroll != null ||
   963	                        mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) != null
   964	                    val wasScrollingDropdown = mockDropdownScrollMoved
   965	                    val wasPressingRememberMe = pressedRememberMe
   966	                    val wasPressingBackChevron = pressedBackChevron
   967	                    val wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget != null
   968	                    val wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit
   969	                    val wasDraggingCreateAvatar = activeMockCreateAvatarDrag
   970	                    val createAvatarDragMoved = mockCreateAvatarDragMoved
   971	                    val createAvatarDragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
   972	                    val createAvatarDragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress()
   973	                    val pressedDropdown = pressedMockDropdown
   974	                    val pressedOption = pressedMockDropdownOption
   975	                    val pressedCreateAvatarNav = pressedMockCreateAvatarNav
   976	                    val pressedForgotRecoverySelection = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget
   977	                    val shouldSelectDropdownOption = !wasScrollingDropdown &&
   978	                        pressedOption != null &&
   979	                        pressedOption == releasedDropdownOption
   980	                    val shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null &&
   981	                        pressedForgotRecoverySelection == releasedForgotRecoveryTarget
   982	                    val shouldToggleDropdown = pressedDropdown != null && pressedDropdown == releasedDropdown
   983	                    val shouldToggleRememberMe = wasPressingRememberMe && releasedRememberMe
   984	                    val shouldCloseMockSignIn = wasPressingBackChevron && releasedBackChevron
   985	                    val shouldSubmitForgotPassword = wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit && releasedForgotPasswordSubmit
   986	                    val shouldNavigateCreateAvatar = pressedCreateAvatarNav != null &&
   987	                        !createAvatarDragMoved &&
   988	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == releasedCreateAvatarNav
   989	                    val shouldSwipeCreateAvatar = wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
   990	                        createAvatarDragMoved &&
   991	                        createAvatarDragDirection != null &&
   992	                        createAvatarDragProgress >= MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_DRAG_COMMIT_PROGRESS
   993	                    val shouldFocusField = releasedField != null &&
   994	                        !wasTouchingDropdownOptions &&
   995	                        !wasPressingRememberMe &&
   996	                        !wasPressingBackChevron &&
   997	                        !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget &&
   998	                        !wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   999	                        !wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
  1000	                        pressedDropdown == null &&
  1001	                        pressedOption == null &&
  1002	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == null &&
  1003	                        (pressedMockField == null || pressedMockField == releasedField)
  1004	                    pressedMockField = null
  1005	                    pressedRememberMe = false
  1006	                    pressedBackChevron = false
  1007	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  1008	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  1009	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  1010	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  1011	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
  1012	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
  1013	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  1014	                    this.pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  1015	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  1016	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  1017	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  1018	                    super.performClick()
  1019	                    if (shouldCloseMockSignIn) {
  1020	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
  1021	                    } else if (shouldSwipeCreateAvatar && createAvatarDragDirection != null) {
  1022	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1023	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1024	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(createAvatarDragDirection, createAvatarDragProgress)
  1025	                    } else if (shouldNavigateCreateAvatar && pressedCreateAvatarNav != null) {
  1026	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1027	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1028	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(pressedCreateAvatarNav)
  1029	                    } else if (wasDraggingCreateAvatar) {
  1030	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1031	                    } else if (shouldSelectDropdownOption && pressedOption != null) {
  1032	                        setMockDropdownValue(pressedOption)
  1033	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1034	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1035	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1036	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1037	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1038	                    } else if (shouldToggleDropdown && pressedDropdown != null) {
  1039	                        expandedMockDropdown = if (expandedMockDropdown == pressedDropdown) null else pressedDropdown
  1040	                        expandedMockDropdown?.let { prepareMockDropdownScroll(it) }
  1041	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1042	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1043	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1044	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1045	                    } else if (shouldToggleRememberMe) {
  1046	                        rememberMeChecked = !rememberMeChecked
  1047	                        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  1048	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1049	                    } else if (shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget && pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null) {
  1050	                        setMockForgotRecoveryTarget(pressedForgotRecoverySelection)
  1051	                    } else if (shouldSubmitForgotPassword) {
  1052	                        startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  1053	                    } else if (shouldFocusField && releasedField != null) {
  1054	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1055	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1056	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1057	                        focusMockSignInField(releasedField)
  1058	                    } else if (wasTouchingDropdownOptions || wasScrollingDropdown) {
  1059	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1060	                    } else if (!wasPressingRememberMe && !wasPressingBackChevron && !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget) {
  1061	                        if (expandedMockDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1062	                            expandedMockDropdown = null
  1063	                            restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1064	                            restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1065	                        }
  1066	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1067	                    } else {
  1068	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1069	                    }
  1070	                    return true
  1071	                }
  1072	                val shouldOpenMockCreateAccount = pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
  1073	                    settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1074	                val shouldOpenMockSignIn = pressedSignInPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1075	                val shouldOpenMockForgotPassword = pressedForgotPasswordPrompt &&
  1076	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1077	                val releasedAvatarProfile = settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
  1078	                val swipeDirection = portraitProfileSwipeDirection()
  1079	                val swipeProgress = portraitProfileDragProgress()
  1080	                val shouldSwipeProfile = isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  1081	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null &&
  1082	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved &&
  1083	                    swipeProgress >= PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_COMMIT_PROGRESS &&
  1084	                    swipeDirection != null
  1085	                val shouldNavigate = !shouldSwipeProfile && !portraitProfileSwipeMoved &&
  1086	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null && pressedAvatarProfile == releasedAvatarProfile
  1087	                clickAvatarProfile = pressedAvatarProfile
  1088	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
  1089	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
  1090	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
  1091	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
  1092	                if (shouldSwipeProfile && swipeDirection != null) {
  1093	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
  1094	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1095	                    portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1096	                    startPortraitProfileCarousel(swipeDirection, swipeProgress)
  1097	                    true
  1098	                } else if (shouldOpenMockCreateAccount) {
  1099	                    super.performClick()
  1100	                    openMockCreateAccountScreen()
  1101	                    true
  1102	                } else if (shouldOpenMockSignIn) {
  1103	                    super.performClick()
  1104	                    openMockSignInScreen()
  1105	                    true
  1106	                } else if (shouldOpenMockForgotPassword) {
  1107	                    super.performClick()
  1108	                    openMockForgotPasswordScreen()
  1109	                    true
  1110	                } else if (shouldNavigate) {
  1111	                    performClick()
  1112	                    true
  1113	                } else {
  1114	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
  1115	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1116	                    portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1117	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1118	                    false
  1119	                }
  1120	            }
  1121	            MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
  1122	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
  1123	                clickAvatarProfile = null
  1124	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
  1125	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
  1126	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
  1127	                pressedMockField = null
  1128	                pressedRememberMe = false
  1129	                pressedBackChevron = false
  1130	                pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  1131	                activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  1132	                mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  1133	                mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  1134	                lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
  1135	                pressedMockDropdown = null
  1136	                pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  1137	                pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  1138	                pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  1139	                activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  1140	                mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  1141	                mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  1142	                portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
  1143	                portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1144	                portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1145	                false
  1146	            }
  1147	            else -> pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
  1148	                pressedSignInPrompt ||
  1149	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
  1150	                pressedAvatarProfile != null ||
  1151	                mockSignInStartMillis != null
  1152	        }
  1153	    }
  1154	
  1155	    override fun onCheckIsTextEditor(): Boolean {
  1156	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && !isMockSignInClosing()
  1157	    }
  1158	
  1159	    override fun onCreateInputConnection(outAttrs: EditorInfo): InputConnection? {
  1160	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return null
  1161	        outAttrs.inputType = when (field) {
  1162	            MockSignInField.Password,
  1163	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_PASSWORD
  1164	            MockSignInField.Email -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_EMAIL_ADDRESS
  1165	            MockSignInField.Month,
  1166	            MockSignInField.Day,
  1167	            MockSignInField.Year -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_NUMBER
  1168	            else -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_NORMAL
  1169	        }
  1170	        outAttrs.imeOptions = if (field == activeMockFields().lastOrNull()) {
  1171	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE
  1172	        } else {
  1173	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_NEXT
  1174	        }
  1175	        val textLength = mockSignInFieldText(field).length
  1176	        outAttrs.initialSelStart = textLength
  1177	        outAttrs.initialSelEnd = textLength
  1178	        return MockSignInInputConnection()
  1179	    }
  1180	
  1181	    override fun onKeyDown(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1182	        if (focusedMockField != null) {
  1183	            return handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode, event) || super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  1184	        }
  1185	        return super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  1186	    }
  1187	
  1188	    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
  1189	    override fun onKeyMultiple(keyCode: Int, repeatCount: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1190	        val characters = event.getCharacters()
  1191	        if (focusedMockField != null && !characters.isNullOrEmpty()) {
  1192	            return appendMockSignInInput(characters)
  1193	        }
  1194	        return super.onKeyMultiple(keyCode, repeatCount, event)
  1195	    }
  1196	
  1197	    override fun performClick(): Boolean {
  1198	        super.performClick()
  1199	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return false
  1200	        val profile = clickAvatarProfile ?: return false
  1201	        onAvatarSelected?.invoke(profile)
  1202	        clickAvatarProfile = null
  1203	        return true
  1204	    }

  1206	    private fun drawSettlingLogo(
  1207	        canvas: Canvas,
  1208	        stageLeft: Float,
  1209	        stageTop: Float,
  1210	        stageScale: Float,
  1211	        progress: Float,
  1212	        alpha: Float
  1213	    ) {
  1214	        val logoEntry = bouncySegmentMs(progress, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
  1215	        val finalProgress = easedSegmentMs(progress, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
  1216	        val focusShiftY = mockLogoFocusShiftY()
  1217	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  1218	        val portraitLogo = Bounds(
  1219	            (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - 600f * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE) / 2f +
  1220	                PORTRAIT_LOGO_VISIBLE_CENTER_OFFSET_X,
  1221	            portraitLogoTop(),
  1222	            600f * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE,
  1223	            LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE
  1224	        )
  1225	        val portraitCenteredLogo = Bounds(
  1226	            x = portraitLogo.x + portraitLogo.w / 2f -
  1227	                portraitLogo.w * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE / 2f,
  1228	            y = portraitStackCenter(0.5f) -
  1229	                portraitLogo.h * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE / 2f,
  1230	            w = portraitLogo.w * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE,
  1231	            h = portraitLogo.h * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE
  1232	        )
  1233	        val base = offsetBounds(
  1234	            lerpBounds(Bounds(222f, 150f, 750f, 535f), portraitCenteredLogo, portraitAmount),
  1235	            focusShiftY
  1236	        )
  1237	        val final = offsetBounds(
  1238	            lerpBounds(Bounds(297f, LOGO_FINAL_TOP, 600f, LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT), portraitLogo, portraitAmount),
  1239	            focusShiftY
  1240	        )
  1241	
  1242	        if (logoEntry >= 0.999f) {
  1243	            drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoCombined, lerpBounds(base, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1244	            return
  1245	        }
  1246	
  1247	        val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(447f, 1037f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
  1248	        val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(453f, -417f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
  1249	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoSimple, lerpBounds(simpleEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1250	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoEyes, lerpBounds(eyesEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1251	    }
  1252	
  1253	    private fun drawPortraitProfile(
  1254	        canvas: Canvas,
  1255	        stage: StageMetrics,
  1256	        yOffset: Float,
  1257	        alpha: Float
  1258	    ) {
  1259	        val slideDistance = portraitAvatarSize()
  1260	        val dragDirection = portraitProfileSwipeDirection()
  1261	        val dragProgress = portraitProfileDragProgress()
  1262	        val carouselProgress = portraitProfileCarouselProgress()
  1263	        val carouselDirection = portraitProfileCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  1264	
  1265	        if (portraitProfileSwipeMoved && dragDirection != null && !isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()) {
  1266	            val direction = dragDirection.stageDirection
  1267	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1268	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * (1f - dragProgress), portraitProfileIndex,
  1269	                -direction * dragProgress * slideDistance
  1270	            )
  1271	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1272	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * dragProgress, portraitProfileIndexForDirection(dragDirection),
  1273	                direction * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance
  1274	            )
  1275	        } else if (isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()) {
  1276	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1277	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * (1f - carouselProgress), portraitProfileCarouselFromIndex,
  1278	                -carouselDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance
  1279	            )
  1280	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1281	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * carouselProgress, portraitProfileCarouselToIndex,
  1282	                carouselDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance
  1283	            )
  1284	        } else {
  1285	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha, portraitProfileIndex, 0f)
  1286	        }
  1287	    }

  1290	        canvas: Canvas,
  1291	        stage: StageMetrics,
  1292	        yOffset: Float,
  1293	        alpha: Float
  1294	    ) {
  1295	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  1296	            drawPortraitProfile(canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha)

  1298	        }
  1299	
  1300	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  1301	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveAvatar, Bounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + yOffset, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1302	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinAvatar, Bounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + yOffset, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1303	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyAvatar, Bounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + yOffset, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1304	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestAvatar, Bounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + yOffset, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1305	
  1306	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveName, Bounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + yOffset, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1307	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinName, Bounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + yOffset, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1308	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyName, Bounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + yOffset, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1309	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestName, Bounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + yOffset, 200f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)

  1355	                portraitProfileNameTop() + yOffset,
  1356	                nameWidth,
  1357	                portraitProfileNameHeight()
  1358	            ),
  1359	            stage.left,
  1360	            stage.top,
  1361	            stage.scale,
  1362	            alpha
  1363	        )
  1364	    }

  1366	    private fun drawIntroBackground(canvas: Canvas, solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float) {
  1367	        val standardSignInBackground = standardSignInBackgroundForSize()
  1368	        if (gradientProgress <= 0.001f) {
  1369	            canvas.drawColor(lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress))
  1370	            return
  1371	        }
  1372	
  1373	        val solid = lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress)
  1374	        backgroundPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  1375	            0f,
  1376	            0f,
  1377	            0f,
  1378	            height.toFloat(),
  1379	            intArrayOf(
  1380	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_TOP, gradientProgress),
  1381	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_CENTER, gradientProgress),
  1382	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_BOTTOM, gradientProgress)
  1383	            ),
  1384	            null,
  1385	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  1386	        )
  1387	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1388	        backgroundPaint.shader = null
  1389	
  1390	        if (standardSignInBackground != null) {
  1391	            drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas, gradientProgress)
  1392	            drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas, standardSignInBackground, gradientProgress)
  1393	        }
  1394	    }
  1395	
  1396	    private fun drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas: Canvas, alpha: Float) {
  1397	        backgroundPaint.color = COLOR_FRAME_1
  1398	        backgroundPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1399	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1400	        backgroundPaint.alpha = 255
  1401	    }
  1402	
  1403	    private fun drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas: Canvas, bitmap: Bitmap, alpha: Float) {
  1404	        tempRect.set(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat())
  1405	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * STANDARD_BACKGROUND_SETTLED_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1406	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1407	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1408	    }
  1409	
  1410	    private fun drawFigmaBitmap(
  1411	        canvas: Canvas,
  1412	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  1413	        bounds: Bounds,
  1414	        stageLeft: Float,
  1415	        stageTop: Float,
  1416	        stageScale: Float,
  1417	        alpha: Float
  1418	    ) {
  1419	        tempRect.set(
  1420	            stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale,
  1421	            stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale,
  1422	            stageLeft + (bounds.x + bounds.w) * stageScale,
  1423	            stageTop + (bounds.y + bounds.h) * stageScale
  1424	        )
  1425	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1426	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1427	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1428	    }

  1430	    private fun drawAccountPrompt(
  1431	        canvas: Canvas,
  1432	        stageLeft: Float,
  1433	        stageTop: Float,
  1434	        stageScale: Float,
  1435	        yOffset: Float,
  1436	        alpha: Float
  1437	    ) {
  1438	        if (usesPortraitStackedAuthLayout(activeMockFlow) ||
  1439	            usesPortraitStackedAuthLayout(outgoingMockFlow)
  1440	        ) {
  1441	            return
  1442	        }
  1443	        val isLandscape = width > height
  1444	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  1445	        val textSize = lerpFloat(
  1446	            signInAccountPromptTextSize(),
  1447	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * PORTRAIT_ACTION_SCALE,
  1448	            portraitAmount
  1449	        ) * stageScale
  1450	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1451	        accountPromptPaint.textSize = textSize
  1452	        formTitlePaint.textSize = textSize
  1453	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1454	        formTitlePaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1455	
  1456	        val accountPromptShiftY = if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1457	        val standardCenterY = stageTop +
  1458	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + accountPromptShiftY + yOffset) * stageScale
  1459	        val secondaryMetrics = accountPromptPaint.fontMetrics
  1460	        val primaryMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1461	        fun promptCenterY(portraitCenterY: Float): Float = lerpFloat(
  1462	            standardCenterY,
  1463	            stageTop + (portraitCenterY + yOffset) * stageScale,
  1464	            portraitAmount
  1465	        )
  1466	        val createCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptCreateCenterY())
  1467	        val signInCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptSignInCenterY())
  1468	        val forgotCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptForgotCenterY())
  1469	        val createBaselineY = createCenterY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1470	        val signInBaselineY = signInCenterY - (primaryMetrics.ascent + primaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1471	        val forgotBaselineY = forgotCenterY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1472	        val portraitCenterX = FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH / 2f
  1473	        if (
  1474	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  1475	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
  1476	        ) {
  1477	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1478	            canvas.drawText(
  1479	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT,
  1480	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(accountPromptCreateCenterX(), portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1481	                createBaselineY,
  1482	                accountPromptPaint
  1483	            )
  1484	        }
  1485	        if (
  1486	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn &&
  1487	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn
  1488	        ) {
  1489	            formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1490	            canvas.drawText(
  1491	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1492	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X, portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1493	                signInBaselineY,
  1494	                formTitlePaint
  1495	            )
  1496	        }
  1497	        if (
  1498	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  1499	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  1500	        ) {
  1501	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1502	            canvas.drawText(
  1503	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT,
  1504	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(accountPromptForgotCenterX(), portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1505	                forgotBaselineY,
  1506	                accountPromptPaint
  1507	            )
  1508	        }
  1509	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = 255
  1510	        accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1511	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1512	    }

  1514	    private fun drawMockSignInForm(
  1515	        canvas: Canvas,
  1516	        stageLeft: Float,
  1517	        stageTop: Float,
  1518	        stageScale: Float,
  1519	        progress: Float,
  1520	        formFocusMotion: Float,
  1521	        updateLabelAnimation: Boolean = true
  1522	    ) {
  1523	        if (updateLabelAnimation) {
  1524	            updateMockFormLabelAnimation()
  1525	        }
  1526	
  1527	        val alpha = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1528	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1529	            drawMockCreateAccountForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1530	            return
  1531	        }
  1532	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  1533	            drawMockForgotPasswordForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1534	            return
  1535	        }
  1536	
  1537	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha)
  1538	        val fieldFillAlpha = (alpha * 26f).roundToInt()
  1539	        val fieldStrokeAlpha = (alpha * 185f).roundToInt()
  1540	        val baseStage = StageMetrics(stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale)
  1541	        val usernameY = mockPrimaryFormY() + yOffset
  1542	        val passwordY = usernameY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + mockFormFieldGap()
  1543	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  1544	        val contentStage = mockSignInContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  1545	        val contentLeft = contentStage.left
  1546	        val contentTop = contentStage.top
  1547	        val contentScale = contentStage.scale
  1548	        val fieldX = mockFormX()
  1549	
  1550	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fieldFillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1551	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(fieldStrokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1552	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * contentScale
  1553	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * contentScale
  1554	
  1555	        val rememberMeY = passwordY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TOP_GAP + focusShiftY
  1556	        val titleFieldY = usernameY + if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) 0f else focusShiftY
  1557	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  1558	            canvas.save()
  1559	            canvas.clipRect(
  1560	                0f,
  1561	                stageTop + portraitLogoVisibleBottom() * stageScale,
  1562	                width.toFloat(),
  1563	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(baseStage)
  1564	            )
  1565	        }
  1566	        drawMockSignInTitle(canvas, titleFieldY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1567	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  1568	            canvas.restore()
  1569	            canvas.save()
  1570	            canvas.clipRect(
  1571	                0f,
  1572	                portraitAuthViewportTopPx(baseStage),
  1573	                width.toFloat(),
  1574	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(baseStage)
  1575	            )
  1576	        }
  1577	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Username, fieldX, usernameY + focusShiftY, contentLeft, contentTop, contentScale, alpha)
  1578	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Password, fieldX, passwordY + focusShiftY, contentLeft, contentTop, contentScale, alpha)
  1579	        drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  1580	            canvas,
  1581	            fieldX,
  1582	            rememberMeY,
  1583	            contentLeft,
  1584	            contentTop,
  1585	            contentScale,
  1586	            alpha
  1587	        )
  1588	        drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1589	            canvas,
  1590	            contentLeft,
  1591	            contentTop,
  1592	            contentScale,
  1593	            alpha,
  1594	            yOffset,
  1595	            focusShiftY
  1596	        )
  1597	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  1598	            canvas.restore()
  1599	        }
  1600	
  1601	        formLabelPaint.alpha = 255
  1602	        formInputPaint.alpha = 255
  1603	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1604	        formCaretPaint.alpha = 255
  1605	    }

  1607	    private fun drawMockCreateAccountForm(

  1609	        stageLeft: Float,
  1610	        stageTop: Float,
  1611	        stageScale: Float,
  1612	        alpha: Float,
  1613	        formFocusMotion: Float
  1614	    ) {

  1617	        val fieldStrokeAlpha = (alpha * 185f).roundToInt()
  1618	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)

  1620	        val contentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  1621	        val contentLeft = contentStage.left

  1623	        val contentScale = contentStage.scale

  1636	                stageTop + portraitLogoVisibleBottom() * stageScale,
  1637	                width.toFloat(),
  1638	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(baseStage)
  1639	            )
  1640	        }
  1641	        drawMockCreateAccountTitle(canvas, titleFieldY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1642	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  1643	            canvas.restore()
  1644	        }
  1645	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  1646	            canvas.save()
  1647	            canvas.clipRect(
  1648	                0f,
  1649	                portraitAuthViewportTopPx(baseStage),
  1650	                width.toFloat(),
  1651	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(baseStage)
  1652	            )
  1653	        }
  1654	        activeMockFields().forEach { field ->
  1655	            drawMockSignInField(
  1656	                canvas,
  1657	                field,
  1658	                mockActiveFieldX(field),
  1659	                mockActiveFieldY(field, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1660	                contentLeft,
  1661	                contentTop,
  1662	                contentScale,
  1663	                alpha,
  1664	                mockActiveFieldWidth(field)
  1665	            )
  1666	        }
  1667	        drawMockDropdownControl(
  1668	            canvas,
  1669	            MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier,
  1670	            mockDropdownX(MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier),
  1671	            mockDropdownY(MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1672	            contentLeft,
  1673	            contentTop,
  1674	            contentScale,
  1675	            alpha
  1676	        )
  1677	        drawMockCreateBirthdayLabel(
  1678	            canvas,
  1679	            mockDropdownX(MockDropdown.Month),
  1680	            mockCreateBirthdayLabelY(yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1681	            contentLeft,
  1682	            contentTop,
  1683	            contentScale,
  1684	            alpha
  1685	        )
  1686	        listOf(MockDropdown.Month, MockDropdown.Day, MockDropdown.Year).forEach { dropdown ->
  1687	            drawMockDropdownControl(
  1688	                canvas,
  1689	                dropdown,
  1690	                mockDropdownX(dropdown),
  1691	                mockDropdownY(dropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1692	                contentLeft,
  1693	                contentTop,
  1694	                contentScale,
  1695	                alpha,
  1696	                mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)
  1697	            )
  1698	        }
  1699	        if (!isMockCreateWelcomeTransition()) {
  1700	            drawMockCreateAccountAvatarText(
  1701	                canvas,
  1702	                mockCreateAvatarColumnX(),
  1703	                mockCreateAvatarSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1704	                contentLeft,
  1705	                contentTop,
  1706	                contentScale,
  1707	                alpha
  1708	            )
  1709	        }
  1710	        drawMockDropdownOptions(canvas, contentLeft, contentTop, contentScale, alpha)
  1711	        drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1712	            canvas,
  1713	            contentLeft,
  1714	            contentTop,
  1715	            contentScale,
  1716	            alpha,
  1717	            yOffset,
  1718	            focusShiftY
  1719	        )
  1720	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  1721	            canvas.restore()
  1722	        }
  1723	
  1724	        formLabelPaint.alpha = 255
  1725	        formInputPaint.alpha = 255
  1726	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1727	        formCaretPaint.alpha = 255
  1728	    }

  1730	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordForm(
  1731	        canvas: Canvas,

  1862	
  1863	    private fun drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1864	        canvas: Canvas,
  1865	        stageLeft: Float,
  1866	        stageTop: Float,
  1867	        stageScale: Float,
  1868	        alpha: Float,
  1869	        yOffset: Float,
  1870	        focusShiftY: Float
  1871	    ) {
  1872	        val dimProgress = mockInputDimProgress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1873	        if (dimProgress <= 0.01f) return
  1874	
  1875	        inputDimPaint.alpha = (dimProgress * MOCK_INPUT_DIM_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1876	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), inputDimPaint)
  1877	        inputDimPaint.alpha = 255
  1878	
  1879	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 26f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1880	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 185f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1881	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1882	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  1883	
  1884	        val activeDropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: mockInputDimActiveDropdown
  1885	        if (activeDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1886	            drawMockDropdownControl(
  1887	                canvas,
  1888	                activeDropdown,
  1889	                mockDropdownX(activeDropdown),
  1890	                mockDropdownY(activeDropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1891	                stageLeft,
  1892	                stageTop,
  1893	                stageScale,
  1894	                alpha,
  1895	                mockDropdownWidth(activeDropdown)
  1896	            )
  1897	            if (expandedMockDropdown == activeDropdown) {
  1898	                drawMockDropdownOptions(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1899	            }
  1900	            return
  1901	        }
  1902	
  1903	        val activeField = focusedMockField ?: mockInputDimActiveField ?: return
  1904	        drawMockSignInField(
  1905	            canvas,
  1906	            activeField,
  1907	            mockActiveFieldX(activeField),
  1908	            mockActiveFieldY(activeField, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1909	            stageLeft,
  1910	            stageTop,
  1911	            stageScale,
  1912	            alpha,
  1913	            mockActiveFieldWidth(activeField)
  1914	        )
  1915	    }

  1917	    private fun drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
  1918	        canvas: Canvas,
  1919	        flow: MockAccountFlow,
  1920	        stageLeft: Float,
  1921	        stageTop: Float,
  1922	        stageScale: Float,
  1923	        progress: Float,
  1924	        formFocusMotion: Float,
  1925	        updateLabelAnimation: Boolean
  1926	    ) {
  1927	        val currentFlow = activeMockFlow
  1928	        activeMockFlow = flow
  1929	        try {
  1930	            drawMockSignInForm(
  1931	                canvas,
  1932	                stageLeft,
  1933	                stageTop,
  1934	                stageScale,
  1935	                progress,
  1936	                formFocusMotion,
  1937	                updateLabelAnimation
  1938	            )
  1939	        } finally {
  1940	            activeMockFlow = currentFlow
  1941	        }
  1942	    }

  1944	    private fun drawMockSignInTitle(
  1945	        canvas: Canvas,
  1946	        fieldTopY: Float,
  1947	        stageLeft: Float,
  1948	        stageTop: Float,
  1949	        stageScale: Float,
  1950	        alpha: Float
  1951	    ) {
  1952	        val motion = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1953	        val portrait = isPortraitIntroLayout()
  1954	        val portraitTextSize = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * PORTRAIT_ACTION_SCALE * stageScale
  1955	        val sourceTextSize = if (portrait) portraitTextSize else signInAccountPromptTextSize() * stageScale
  1956	        val targetTextSize = if (portrait) portraitTextSize else MOCK_FORM_TITLE_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1957	        val sourceCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  1958	        val targetCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  1959	        val sourceCenterY = stageTop + (if (portrait) {
  1960	            portraitPromptSignInCenterY()
  1961	        } else {
  1962	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY()
  1963	        }) * stageScale
  1964	        formTitlePaint.textSize = sourceTextSize
  1965	        val sourceMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1966	        val sourceBaselineY = sourceCenterY - (sourceMetrics.ascent + sourceMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1967	        val targetBaselineY = mockFormTitleTargetBaseline(
  1968	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1969	            fieldTopY,
  1970	            stageTop,
  1971	            stageScale,
  1972	            targetTextSize
  1973	        )
  1974	
  1975	        formTitlePaint.textSize = lerpFloat(sourceTextSize, targetTextSize, motion)
  1976	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1977	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1978	        canvas.drawText(
  1979	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1980	            lerpFloat(sourceCenterX, targetCenterX, motion),
  1981	            lerpFloat(sourceBaselineY, targetBaselineY, motion),
  1982	            formTitlePaint
  1983	        )
  1984	    }

  2077	
  2078	    private fun mockFormTitleTargetBaseline(
  2079	        title: String,
  2080	        contentTopY: Float,
  2081	        stageTop: Float,
  2082	        stageScale: Float,
  2083	        targetTextSize: Float
  2084	    ): Float {
  2085	        formTitlePaint.textSize = targetTextSize
  2086	        val titlePath = Path()
  2087	        val titleBounds = RectF()
  2088	        formTitlePaint.getTextPath(title, 0, title.length, 0f, 0f, titlePath)
  2089	        titlePath.computeBounds(titleBounds, true)
  2090	        val contentScaleRatio = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) portraitCreateContentScale() else 1f
  2091	        return stageTop + contentTopY * stageScale -
  2092	            mockFormFieldGap() * 2f * stageScale * contentScaleRatio -
  2093	            titleBounds.bottom +
  2094	            MOCK_FORM_TITLE_OPTICAL_BOTTOM_OFFSET * stageScale * contentScaleRatio
  2095	    }
  2096	
  2097	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButton(
  2098	        canvas: Canvas,
  2099	        x: Float,
  2100	        y: Float,
  2101	        stageLeft: Float,
  2102	        stageTop: Float,
  2103	        stageScale: Float,
  2104	        alpha: Float,
  2105	        buttonWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2106	    ) {
  2107	        tempRect.set(
  2108	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2109	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2110	            stageLeft + (x + buttonWidth) * stageScale,
  2111	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2112	        )
  2113	        val radius = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_RADIUS * stageScale
  2114	        val submissionState = forgotPasswordSubmissionState
  2115	        val enabled = mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  2116	        val pressed = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit && enabled
  2117	        val fillAlpha = when {
  2118	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 86f
  2119	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 72f
  2120	            pressed -> 92f
  2121	            enabled -> 68f
  2122	            else -> 18f
  2123	        }
  2124	        val strokeAlpha = when {
  2125	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 245f
  2126	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 228f
  2127	            pressed -> 238f
  2128	            enabled -> 210f
  2129	            else -> 118f
  2130	        }
  2131	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  2132	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * fillAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2133	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * strokeAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2134	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2135	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2136	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2137	        if (submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading) {
  2138	            drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(canvas, tempRect, radius, alpha)
  2139	        }
  2140	
  2141	        formTitlePaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2142	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2143	        val metrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  2144	        val baselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2145	        val maxTextWidth = buttonWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  2146	        val showCheck = submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  2147	        val checkSize = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_SIZE * stageScale else 0f
  2148	        val checkGap = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_GAP * stageScale else 0f
  2149	        val textMaxWidth = (maxTextWidth - checkSize - checkGap).coerceAtLeast(maxTextWidth * 0.5f)
  2150	        val buttonText = trailingFittingText(
  2151	            mockForgotPasswordButtonText(),
  2152	            if (showCheck) textMaxWidth else maxTextWidth,
  2153	            formTitlePaint
  2154	        )
  2155	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * when {
  2156	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 226f
  2157	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 255f
  2158	            enabled -> 255f
  2159	            else -> 138f
  2160	        }).roundToInt()
  2161	        formTitlePaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2162	        val textX = if (showCheck) {
  2163	            val textWidth = formTitlePaint.measureText(buttonText)
  2164	            val groupWidth = checkSize + checkGap + textWidth
  2165	            val groupLeft = tempRect.centerX() - groupWidth / 2f
  2166	            val checkLeft = groupLeft
  2167	            val checkCenterY = tempRect.centerY()
  2168	            backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2169	            backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 2.1f * stageScale
  2170	            canvas.drawLine(
  2171	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.18f,
  2172	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.03f,
  2173	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  2174	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  2175	                backChevronPaint
  2176	            )
  2177	            canvas.drawLine(
  2178	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  2179	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  2180	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.84f,
  2181	                checkCenterY - checkSize * 0.28f,
  2182	                backChevronPaint
  2183	            )
  2184	            groupLeft + checkSize + checkGap + textWidth / 2f
  2185	        } else {
  2186	            tempRect.centerX()
  2187	        }
  2188	        canvas.drawText(
  2189	            buttonText,
  2190	            textX,
  2191	            baselineY,
  2192	            formTitlePaint
  2193	        )
  2194	    }

  2196	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(
  2197	        canvas: Canvas,
  2198	        buttonRect: RectF,
  2199	        radius: Float,
  2200	        alpha: Float
  2201	    ) {
  2202	        val progress = mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress()
  2203	        val sweepWidth = buttonRect.width() * 0.44f
  2204	        val sweepCenterX = buttonRect.left + buttonRect.width() * (progress * 1.35f - 0.18f)
  2205	        val sweepLeft = sweepCenterX - sweepWidth / 2f
  2206	        val sweepRight = sweepCenterX + sweepWidth / 2f
  2207	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  2208	            sweepLeft,
  2209	            buttonRect.top,
  2210	            sweepRight,
  2211	            buttonRect.top,
  2212	            intArrayOf(
  2213	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255),
  2214	                Color.argb((alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 54f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255),
  2215	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255)
  2216	            ),
  2217	            floatArrayOf(0f, 0.5f, 1f),
  2218	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  2219	        )
  2220	        canvas.save()
  2221	        tempPath.reset()
  2222	        tempPath.addRoundRect(buttonRect, radius, radius, Path.Direction.CW)
  2223	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  2224	        canvas.drawRect(sweepLeft, buttonRect.top, sweepRight, buttonRect.bottom, formFieldFillPaint)
  2225	        canvas.restore()
  2226	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  2227	    }

  2286	    private fun drawMockForgotRecoveryOption(
  2287	        canvas: Canvas,
  2288	        target: MockForgotRecoveryTarget,
  2289	        left: Float,
  2290	        top: Float,
  2291	        boxWidth: Float,
  2292	        stageScale: Float,
  2293	        alpha: Float
  2294	    ) {
  2295	        val right = left + boxWidth
  2296	        val bottom = top + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_HEIGHT * stageScale
  2297	        tempRect.set(left, top, right, bottom)
  2298	        val selected = forgotRecoveryTarget == target
  2299	        val pressed = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == target
  2300	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * when {
  2301	            pressed -> 82f
  2302	            selected -> 64f
  2303	            else -> 14f
  2304	        }).roundToInt()
  2305	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * when {
  2306	            pressed -> 245f
  2307	            selected -> 235f
  2308	            else -> 142f
  2309	        }).roundToInt()
  2310	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2311	
  2312	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2313	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(strokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2314	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2315	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2316	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2317	
  2318	        formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_OPTION_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2319	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (selected || pressed) 255f else 178f).roundToInt()
  2320	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2321	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2322	        canvas.drawText(
  2323	            target.displayText,
  2324	            tempRect.centerX(),
  2325	            tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  2326	            formInputPaint
  2327	        )
  2328	    }
