  2330	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordBodyParagraph(
  2331	        canvas: Canvas,
  2332	        text: String,
  2333	        centerX: Float,
  2334	        topY: Float,
  2335	        maxWidth: Float,
  2336	        stageLeft: Float,
  2337	        stageTop: Float,
  2338	        stageScale: Float,
  2339	        alpha: Float
  2340	    ): Float {
  2341	        val paragraphs = text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n"))
  2342	        val leadBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  2343	            canvas,
  2344	            paragraphs.firstOrNull().orEmpty(),
  2345	            centerX,
  2346	            topY,
  2347	            maxWidth,
  2348	            stageLeft,
  2349	            stageTop,
  2350	            stageScale,
  2351	            alpha,
  2352	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_TEXT_SIZE,
  2353	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_LINE_HEIGHT
  2354	        )
  2355	        val middleText = paragraphs.getOrNull(1) ?: return leadBottomY
  2356	        val middleBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  2357	            canvas,
  2358	            middleText,
  2359	            centerX,
  2360	            leadBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2361	            maxWidth,
  2362	            stageLeft,
  2363	            stageTop,
  2364	            stageScale,
  2365	            alpha,
  2366	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  2367	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2368	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  2369	        )
  2370	        val bottomText = paragraphs.drop(2).joinToString("\n\n")
  2371	        if (bottomText.isEmpty()) return middleBottomY
  2372	        return drawMockFormParagraph(
  2373	            canvas,
  2374	            bottomText,
  2375	            centerX,
  2376	            middleBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2377	            maxWidth,
  2378	            stageLeft,
  2379	            stageTop,
  2380	            stageScale,
  2381	            alpha,
  2382	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  2383	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2384	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  2385	        )
  2386	    }
  2387	
  2388	    private fun drawMockFormParagraph(
  2389	        canvas: Canvas,
  2390	        text: String,
  2391	        centerX: Float,
  2392	        topY: Float,
  2393	        maxWidth: Float,
  2394	        stageLeft: Float,
  2395	        stageTop: Float,
  2396	        stageScale: Float,
  2397	        alpha: Float,
  2398	        textSize: Float,
  2399	        lineHeight: Float,
  2400	        textAlign: Paint.Align = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2401	    ): Float {
  2402	        formInputPaint.textSize = textSize * stageScale
  2403	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 218f).roundToInt()
  2404	        formInputPaint.textAlign = textAlign
  2405	
  2406	        val lines = wrappedTextLines(text, maxWidth * stageScale, formInputPaint)
  2407	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2408	        val x = when (textAlign) {
  2409	            Paint.Align.LEFT -> stageLeft + (centerX - maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  2410	            Paint.Align.RIGHT -> stageLeft + (centerX + maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  2411	            Paint.Align.CENTER -> stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  2412	        }
  2413	        var baselineY = stageTop + topY * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  2414	        lines.forEach { line ->
  2415	            canvas.drawText(line, x, baselineY, formInputPaint)
  2416	            baselineY += lineHeight * stageScale
  2417	        }
  2418	        return topY + lines.size * lineHeight
  2419	    }
  2420	
  2421	    private fun drawMockCreateAccountAvatarText(
  2422	        canvas: Canvas,
  2423	        columnX: Float,
  2424	        firstFieldY: Float,
  2425	        stageLeft: Float,
  2426	        stageTop: Float,
  2427	        stageScale: Float,
  2428	        alpha: Float
  2429	    ) {
  2430	        val avatarScale = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE
  2431	        val scaledAvatarSize = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * avatarScale
  2432	        val labelBaselineFromTop = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LABEL_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * avatarScale
  2433	        val avatarTopGap = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_TOP_GAP * avatarScale
  2434	        val centerX = stageLeft + (columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f) * stageScale
  2435	        val baselineY = stageTop + (firstFieldY + labelBaselineFromTop) * stageScale
  2436	        drawMockCreateUsername(canvas, centerX, baselineY, stageScale, alpha)
  2437	
  2438	        val avatarLeftX = columnX + (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH - scaledAvatarSize) / 2f
  2439	        val avatarTopY = firstFieldY + labelBaselineFromTop + avatarTopGap
  2440	        val avatarBounds = Bounds(
  2441	            avatarLeftX,
  2442	            avatarTopY,
  2443	            scaledAvatarSize,
  2444	            scaledAvatarSize
  2445	        )
  2446	        val usesSeparatedMotionLayers = isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating() ||
  2447	            (activeMockCreateAvatarDrag && mockCreateAvatarDragMoved)
  2448	        if (usesSeparatedMotionLayers) {
  2449	            val carouselAnimating = isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
  2450	            val motionProgress = if (carouselAnimating) {
  2451	                mockCreateAvatarCarouselProgress()
  2452	            } else {
  2453	                mockCreateAvatarDragProgress()
  2454	            }
  2455	            val startBlend = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(
  2456	                (motionProgress / MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LAYER_BLEND_PROGRESS).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  2457	            )
  2458	            val endBlend = if (carouselAnimating) {
  2459	                FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(
  2460	                    ((motionProgress - (1f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LAYER_BLEND_PROGRESS)) /
  2461	                        MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LAYER_BLEND_PROGRESS).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  2462	                )
  2463	            } else {
  2464	                0f
  2465	            }
  2466	            val effectiveMotionProgress = when {
  2467	                motionProgress <= MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LAYER_BLEND_PROGRESS -> 0f
  2468	                carouselAnimating &&
  2469	                    motionProgress >= 1f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LAYER_BLEND_PROGRESS -> 1f
  2470	                carouselAnimating -> {
  2471	                    (motionProgress - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LAYER_BLEND_PROGRESS) /
  2472	                        (1f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LAYER_BLEND_PROGRESS * 2f)
  2473	                }
  2474	                else -> {
  2475	                    (motionProgress - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LAYER_BLEND_PROGRESS) /
  2476	                        (1f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LAYER_BLEND_PROGRESS)
  2477	                }
  2478	            }.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  2479	            val separatedAlpha = alpha * minOf(startBlend, 1f - endBlend)
  2480	            canvas.save()
  2481	            clipMockCreateAvatarCarousel(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale)
  2482	            drawMockCreateAvatarMotionLayer(
  2483	                canvas, createAvatarLayers.characters, avatarBounds,
  2484	                stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, separatedAlpha, effectiveMotionProgress
  2485	            )
  2486	            canvas.restore()
  2487	            drawFigmaBitmap(
  2488	                canvas,
  2489	                createAvatarLayers.bubble,
  2490	                avatarBounds,
  2491	                stageLeft,
  2492	                stageTop,
  2493	                stageScale,
  2494	                separatedAlpha
  2495	            )
  2496	            canvas.save()
  2497	            clipMockCreateAvatarCarousel(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale)
  2498	            drawMockCreateAvatarMotionLayer(
  2499	                canvas, createAvatarLayers.bottomStrokes, avatarBounds,
  2500	                stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, separatedAlpha, effectiveMotionProgress
  2501	            )
  2502	            canvas.restore()
  2503	            if (startBlend < 1f) {
  2504	                val sourceIndex = if (carouselAnimating) {
  2505	                    mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex
  2506	                } else {
  2507	                    mockCreateAvatarIndex
  2508	                }
  2509	                drawFigmaBitmap(
  2510	                    canvas,
  2511	                    createAvatarBitmaps[sourceIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)],
  2512	                    avatarBounds,
  2513	                    stageLeft,
  2514	                    stageTop,
  2515	                    stageScale,
  2516	                    alpha * (1f - startBlend)
  2517	                )
  2518	            }
  2519	            if (endBlend > 0f) {
  2520	                drawFigmaBitmap(
  2521	                    canvas,
  2522	                    createAvatarBitmaps[
  2523	                        mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
  2524	                    ],
  2525	                    avatarBounds,
  2526	                    stageLeft,
  2527	                    stageTop,
  2528	                    stageScale,
  2529	                    alpha * endBlend
  2530	                )
  2531	            }
  2532	        } else {
  2533	            drawFigmaBitmap(
  2534	                canvas,
  2535	                createAvatarBitmaps[mockCreateAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)],
  2536	                avatarBounds,
  2537	                stageLeft,
  2538	                stageTop,
  2539	                stageScale,
  2540	                alpha
  2541	            )
  2542	        }
  2543	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2544	            canvas,
  2545	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous,
  2546	            avatarBounds,
  2547	            stageLeft,
  2548	            stageTop,
  2549	            stageScale,
  2550	            alpha
  2551	        )
  2552	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2553	            canvas,
  2554	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next,
  2555	            avatarBounds,
  2556	            stageLeft,
  2557	            stageTop,
  2558	            stageScale,
  2559	            alpha
  2560	        )
  2561	    }
  2562	
  2563	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarTransitionOverlay(
  2564	        canvas: Canvas,
  2565	        stage: StageMetrics,
  2566	        mockMotion: Float,
  2567	        formFocusMotion: Float
  2568	    ) {
  2569	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return
  2570	
  2571	        if (isMockCreateWelcomeTransition()) {
  2572	            drawMockCreateWelcomeTransition(
  2573	                canvas,
  2574	                stage,
  2575	                mockMotion.coerceIn(0f, 1f),
  2576	                formFocusMotion
  2577	            )
  2578	            return
  2579	        }
  2580	
  2581	        val outgoingFlow = outgoingMockFlow
  2582	        val transitionProgress = when {
  2583	            outgoingFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount -> {
  2584	                1f - FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFlowSwitchProgress())
  2585	            }
  2586	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount && outgoingFlow != null -> {
  2587	                FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFlowSwitchProgress())
  2588	            }
  2589	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount && isMockSignInAnimating() -> mockMotion
  2590	            else -> return
  2591	        }.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  2592	
  2593	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - transitionProgress)
  2594	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  2595	        val contentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  2596	
  2597	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2598	            canvas.save()
  2599	            canvas.clipRect(
  2600	                0f,
  2601	                portraitAuthViewportTopPx(stage),
  2602	                width.toFloat(),
  2603	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)
  2604	            )
  2605	        }
  2606	        drawMockCreateAccountAvatarText(
  2607	            canvas,
  2608	            mockCreateAvatarColumnX(),
  2609	            mockCreateAvatarSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY),
  2610	            contentStage.left,
  2611	            contentStage.top,
  2612	            contentStage.scale,
  2613	            1f
  2614	        )
  2615	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2616	            canvas.restore()
  2617	        }
  2618	    }
  2619	
  2620	    private fun isMockCreateWelcomeTransition(): Boolean {
  2621	        return isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  2622	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  2623	            outgoingMockFlow == null &&
  2624	            isMockSignInAnimating()
  2625	    }

  2627	    private fun drawMockCreateWelcomeTransition(
  2628	        canvas: Canvas,
  2629	        stage: StageMetrics,
  2630	        motion: Float,
  2631	        formFocusMotion: Float
  2632	    ) {
  2633	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  2634	        val accessoryYOffset = MOCK_SIGN_IN_STACK_EXIT_Y * (1f - motion)
  2635	        val contentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(stage, accessoryYOffset, focusShiftY)
  2636	        val columnX = mockCreateAvatarColumnX()
  2637	        val sectionY = mockCreateAvatarSectionY(accessoryYOffset, focusShiftY)
  2638	        val settledSectionY = mockCreateAvatarSectionY(0f, 0f)
  2639	        val settledContentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(stage, 0f, 0f)
  2640	        val settledAvatarBounds = mockCreateAvatarBounds(columnX, settledSectionY)
  2641	
  2642	        drawWelcomeAvatarRelatedAssetsForCreateTransition(canvas, stage, motion)
  2643	
  2644	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2645	            canvas.save()
  2646	            canvas.clipRect(
  2647	                0f,
  2648	                portraitAuthViewportTopPx(stage),
  2649	                width.toFloat(),
  2650	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)
  2651	            )
  2652	        }
  2653	        val labelBaselineFromTop =
  2654	            MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LABEL_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE
  2655	        drawMockCreateUsername(
  2656	            canvas,
  2657	            contentStage.left + (columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f) * contentStage.scale,
  2658	            contentStage.top + (sectionY + labelBaselineFromTop) * contentStage.scale,
  2659	            contentStage.scale,
  2660	            1f
  2661	        )
  2662	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2663	            canvas,
  2664	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous,
  2665	            settledAvatarBounds,
  2666	            settledContentStage.left,
  2667	            settledContentStage.top,
  2668	            settledContentStage.scale,
  2669	            motion,
  2670	            mockCreateTransitionChevronCenterX(
  2671	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous,
  2672	                settledAvatarBounds,
  2673	                settledContentStage,
  2674	                motion
  2675	            )
  2676	        )
  2677	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2678	            canvas,
  2679	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next,
  2680	            settledAvatarBounds,
  2681	            settledContentStage.left,
  2682	            settledContentStage.top,
  2683	            settledContentStage.scale,
  2684	            motion,
  2685	            mockCreateTransitionChevronCenterX(
  2686	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next,
  2687	                settledAvatarBounds,
  2688	                settledContentStage,
  2689	                motion
  2690	            )
  2691	        )
  2692	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2693	            canvas.restore()
  2694	        }
  2695	
  2696	        val destinationBounds = Bounds(
  2697	            (settledContentStage.left + settledAvatarBounds.x * settledContentStage.scale - stage.left) /
  2698	                stage.scale,
  2699	            (settledContentStage.top + settledAvatarBounds.y * settledContentStage.scale - stage.top) /
  2700	                stage.scale,
  2701	            settledAvatarBounds.w * settledContentStage.scale / stage.scale,
  2702	            settledAvatarBounds.h * settledContentStage.scale / stage.scale
  2703	        )
  2704	        val sourceBounds = mockCreateWelcomeAvatarSourceBounds()
  2705	        val movingBounds = lerpBounds(sourceBounds, destinationBounds, motion)
  2706	        val avatarIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
  2707	
  2708	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  2709	            canvas,
  2710	            createAvatarBitmaps[avatarIndex],
  2711	            movingBounds,
  2712	            stage.left,
  2713	            stage.top,
  2714	            stage.scale,
  2715	            1f
  2716	        )
  2717	    }
  2718	
  2719	    private fun mockCreateTransitionChevronCenterX(
  2720	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  2721	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2722	        contentStage: StageMetrics,
  2723	        motion: Float
  2724	    ): Float {
  2725	        val settledCenterX = contentStage.left +
  2726	            mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * contentStage.scale
  2727	        val halfChevronWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_WIDTH * contentStage.scale / 2f
  2728	        val edgeCenterX = if (direction == MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous) {
  2729	            -halfChevronWidth
  2730	        } else {
  2731	            width.toFloat() + halfChevronWidth
  2732	        }
  2733	        return lerpFloat(edgeCenterX, settledCenterX, motion.coerceIn(0f, 1f))
  2734	    }
  2735	
  2736	    private fun mockCreateWelcomeAvatarSourceBounds(): Bounds {
  2737	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2738	            val avatarSize = portraitAvatarSize()
  2739	            return Bounds(
  2740	                (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - avatarSize) / 2f,
  2741	                portraitAvatarTop(),
  2742	                avatarSize,
  2743	                avatarSize
  2744	            )
  2745	        }
  2746	
  2747	        val source = FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[
  2748	            mockCreateAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
  2749	        ].bounds
  2750	        return Bounds(source.x, source.y + signInStackShiftY(), source.w, source.h)
  2751	    }

  2753	    private fun drawWelcomeAvatarRelatedAssetsForCreateTransition(
  2754	        canvas: Canvas,
  2755	        stage: StageMetrics,
  2756	        motion: Float
  2757	    ) {
  2758	        val exitY = mockCreateRelatedAssetsExitY() * motion
  2759	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2760	            val profile = FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[portraitProfileIndex].profile
  2761	            val nameBitmap = when (profile) {
  2762	                CinerificProfile.Steve -> steveName
  2763	                CinerificProfile.Martin -> martinName
  2764	                CinerificProfile.Janny -> jannyName
  2765	                CinerificProfile.Guest -> guestName
  2766	            }
  2767	            val nameWidth = (if (profile == CinerificProfile.Guest) 200f else 220f) *
  2768	                PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SCALE
  2769	            drawFigmaBitmap(
  2770	                canvas,
  2771	                nameBitmap,
  2772	                Bounds(
  2773	                    (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - nameWidth) / 2f,
  2774	                    portraitProfileNameTop() + exitY,
  2775	                    nameWidth,
  2776	                    portraitProfileNameHeight()
  2777	                ),
  2778	                stage.left,
  2779	                stage.top,
  2780	                stage.scale,
  2781	                1f
  2782	            )
  2783	            return
  2784	        }
  2785	
  2786	        val selectedIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
  2787	        val avatarBitmaps = listOf(steveAvatar, martinAvatar, jannyAvatar, guestAvatar)
  2788	        val nameBitmaps = listOf(steveName, martinName, jannyName, guestName)
  2789	        val nameWidths = listOf(220f, 220f, 220f, 200f)
  2790	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  2791	        FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.forEachIndexed { index, target ->
  2792	            if (index != selectedIndex) {
  2793	                drawFigmaBitmap(
  2794	                    canvas,
  2795	                    avatarBitmaps[index],
  2796	                    Bounds(
  2797	                        target.bounds.x,
  2798	                        target.bounds.y + stackShiftY + exitY,
  2799	                        target.bounds.w,
  2800	                        target.bounds.h
  2801	                    ),
  2802	                    stage.left,
  2803	                    stage.top,
  2804	                    stage.scale,
  2805	                    1f
  2806	                )
  2807	            }
  2808	            drawFigmaBitmap(
  2809	                canvas,
  2810	                nameBitmaps[index],
  2811	                Bounds(
  2812	                    target.bounds.x - if (index == 3) 12f else 22f,
  2813	                    SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + exitY,
  2814	                    nameWidths[index],
  2815	                    72f
  2816	                ),
  2817	                stage.left,
  2818	                stage.top,
  2819	                stage.scale,
  2820	                1f
  2821	            )
  2822	        }
  2823	    }

  2825	    private fun mockCreateRelatedAssetsExitY(): Float {
  2826	        return if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2827	            portraitSafeBottom() - portraitProfileNameTop() + portraitProfileNameHeight()
  2828	        } else {
  2829	            FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT - (SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + signInStackShiftY()) + 72f

  2833	    private fun drawMockCreateUsername(
  2834	        canvas: Canvas,
  2835	        centerX: Float,
  2836	        baselineY: Float,
  2837	        stageScale: Float,
  2838	        alpha: Float
  2839	    ) {
  2840	        if (usernameText.isEmpty()) return
  2841	
  2842	        val maxTextWidth =
  2843	            (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH - MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_MAX_WIDTH_INSET * 2f) * stageScale
  2844	        val preferredTextSize = MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2845	        createUsernameFillPaint.textSize = preferredTextSize
  2846	        val preferredTextWidth = createUsernameFillPaint.measureText(usernameText)
  2847	        val fittedTextSize = if (preferredTextWidth > maxTextWidth && preferredTextWidth > 0f) {
  2848	            preferredTextSize * maxTextWidth / preferredTextWidth
  2849	        } else {
  2850	            preferredTextSize
  2851	        }
  2852	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2853	
  2854	        createUsernameStrokePaint.textSize = fittedTextSize
  2855	        createUsernameStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2856	        createUsernameStrokePaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2857	        createUsernameStrokePaint.setShadowLayer(
  2858	            MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_SHADOW_RADIUS * stageScale,
  2859	            0f,
  2860	            MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_SHADOW_OFFSET_Y * stageScale,
  2861	            Color.argb((paintAlpha * 0.25f).roundToInt(), 0, 0, 0)
  2862	        )
  2863	        canvas.drawText(usernameText, centerX, baselineY, createUsernameStrokePaint)
  2864	        createUsernameStrokePaint.clearShadowLayer()
  2865	
  2866	        createUsernameFillPaint.textSize = fittedTextSize
  2867	        createUsernameFillPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2868	        canvas.drawText(usernameText, centerX, baselineY, createUsernameFillPaint)
  2869	
  2870	        createUsernameStrokePaint.alpha = 255
  2871	        createUsernameFillPaint.alpha = 255
  2872	    }

  2895	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarMotionLayer(
  2896	        canvas: Canvas,
  2897	        bitmaps: List<Bitmap>,
  2898	        bounds: Bounds,
  2899	        stageLeft: Float,
  2900	        stageTop: Float,
  2901	        stageScale: Float,
  2902	        alpha: Float,
  2903	        transitionProgressOverride: Float? = null
  2904	    ) {
  2905	        val carouselProgress = transitionProgressOverride ?: mockCreateAvatarCarouselProgress()
  2906	        val slideDirection = mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  2907	        val slideDistance = mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(bounds.w)
  2908	        val dragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
  2909	        val dragProgress = transitionProgressOverride ?:
  2910	            mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(slideDistance, stageScale)
  2911	        val isDraggingAvatar = activeMockCreateAvatarDrag &&
  2912	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  2913	            dragDirection != null &&
  2914	            !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
  2915	
  2916	        fun draw(index: Int, offsetX: Float) {
  2917	            drawFigmaBitmap(
  2918	                canvas,
  2919	                bitmaps[index.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)],
  2920	                Bounds(bounds.x + offsetX, bounds.y, bounds.w, bounds.h),
  2921	                stageLeft,
  2922	                stageTop,
  2923	                stageScale,
  2924	                alpha
  2925	            )
  2926	        }
  2927	
  2928	        if (isDraggingAvatar && dragDirection != null) {
  2929	            val direction = dragDirection.stageDirection
  2930	            draw(mockCreateAvatarIndex, -direction * dragProgress * slideDistance)
  2931	            draw(mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(dragDirection), direction * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance)
  2932	        } else if (isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()) {
  2933	            draw(mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex, -slideDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance)
  2934	            draw(mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex, slideDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance)
  2935	        } else {
  2936	            draw(mockCreateAvatarIndex, 0f)
  2937	        }
  2938	    }

  2940	    private fun clipMockCreateAvatarCarousel(
  2941	        canvas: Canvas,
  2942	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2943	        stageLeft: Float,
  2944	        stageTop: Float,
  2945	        stageScale: Float
  2946	    ) {
  2947	        tempPath.reset()
  2948	        tempPath.addCircle(
  2949	            stageLeft + (avatarBounds.x + avatarBounds.w / 2f) * stageScale,
  2950	            stageTop + (avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h / 2f) * stageScale,
  2951	            avatarBounds.w * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CLIP_RADIUS_RATIO * stageScale,
  2952	            Path.Direction.CW
  2953	        )
  2954	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  2955	    }

  2957	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2958	        canvas: Canvas,
  2959	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  2960	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2961	        stageLeft: Float,
  2962	        stageTop: Float,
  2963	        stageScale: Float,
  2964	        alpha: Float,
  2965	        centerXOverridePx: Float? = null
  2966	    ) {
  2967	        val centerX = centerXOverridePx ?:
  2968	            stageLeft + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * stageScale
  2969	        val centerY = stageTop + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stageScale
  2970	        val halfWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2971	        val halfHeight = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * stageScale / 2f
  2972	        val pressedAlpha = if (pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == direction) 0.62f else 0.9f
  2973	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * pressedAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  2974	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2975	
  2976	        if (direction == MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous) {
  2977	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX - halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  2978	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY, centerX + halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  2979	        } else {
  2980	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX + halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  2981	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY, centerX - halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  2982	        }
  2983	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2984	    }

  2986	    private fun drawMockDropdownControl(
  2987	        canvas: Canvas,

  2989	        x: Float,
  2990	        y: Float,
  2991	        stageLeft: Float,
  2992	        stageTop: Float,

  3003	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale

  3005	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)

  3018	        } else {
  3019	            0f
  3020	        }

  3031	            )

  3035	            tempRect.left + textInset + iconLaneWidth,

  3037	            formInputPaint
  3038	        )
  3039

  3041	        val centerY = tempRect.centerY() + 2f * stageScale
  3042	        val halfSize = mockDropdownChevronSize(dropdown) * stageScale / 2f
  3043	        backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  3044	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 1.65f * stageScale
  3045	        canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  3046	        canvas.drawLine(centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, centerX + halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  3047	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  3048	    }

  3050	    private fun drawMockDropdownOptions(
  3051	        canvas: Canvas,
  3052	        stageLeft: Float,
  3053	        stageTop: Float,
  3054	        stageScale: Float,
  3055	        alpha: Float
  3056	    ) {
  3057	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return
  3058	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return
  3059	
  3060	        val x = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  3061	        val y = mockDropdownOptionsY(
  3062	            dropdown,
  3063	            MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha),
  3064	            mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  3065	        )
  3066	        val left = stageLeft + x * stageScale
  3067	        val top = stageTop + y * stageScale
  3068	        val right = stageLeft + (x + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stageScale
  3069	        val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stageScale
  3070	        val optionAlpha = 255
  3071	        val optionTextInset = mockDropdownOptionTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  3072	        val scrollbarReservedWidth = if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  3073	            (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stageScale
  3074	        } else {
  3075	            0f
  3076	        }
  3077	
  3078	        val visibleOptions = mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown)
  3079	        visibleOptions.forEachIndexed { index, option ->
  3080	            val optionTop = top + index * optionHeight
  3081	            val optionBottom = optionTop + optionHeight
  3082	            val fillColor = if (option == mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown)) {
  3083	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 76, 12, 94)
  3084	            } else {
  3085	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 14, 2, 18)
  3086	            }
  3087	            tempRect.set(left, optionTop, right, optionBottom)
  3088	            formFieldFillPaint.color = fillColor
  3089	            formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(optionAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  3090	            formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = 1.2f * stageScale
  3091	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldFillPaint)
  3092	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldStrokePaint)
  3093	
  3094	            formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownOptionTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  3095	            formInputPaint.alpha = optionAlpha
  3096	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  3097	            val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  3098	            val membershipIcon = membershipTierArtworkIconForOption(option)
  3099	                ?.takeIf { dropdown == MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier }
  3100	            val iconLaneWidth = if (membershipIcon != null) {
  3101	                (MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_OPTION_ICON_SIZE + MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_ICON_TEXT_GAP) * stageScale
  3102	            } else {
  3103	                0f
  3104	            }
  3105	            if (membershipIcon != null) {
  3106	                drawMembershipTierIcon(
  3107	                    canvas = canvas,
  3108	                    bitmap = membershipIcon,
  3109	                    left = left + optionTextInset,
  3110	                    centerY = (optionTop + optionBottom) / 2f,
  3111	                    iconSize = MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_OPTION_ICON_SIZE * stageScale,
  3112	                    alpha = optionAlpha
  3113	                )
  3114	            }
  3115	            val maxTextWidth = (right - left - optionTextInset - iconLaneWidth - scrollbarReservedWidth)
  3116	                .coerceAtLeast(0f)
  3117	            canvas.drawText(
  3118	                trailingFittingText(option, maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  3119	                left + optionTextInset + iconLaneWidth,
  3120	                (optionTop + optionBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  3121	                formInputPaint
  3122	            )
  3123	        }
  3124	
  3125	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  3126	            drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  3127	                canvas,
  3128	                dropdown,
  3129	                top,
  3130	                right,
  3131	                optionHeight * visibleOptions.size,
  3132	                stageScale,
  3133	                optionAlpha
  3134	            )
  3135	        }
  3136	    }

  3138	    private fun drawMembershipTierIcon(
  3139	        canvas: Canvas,
  3140	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  3141	        left: Float,
  3142	        centerY: Float,
  3143	        iconSize: Float,
  3144	        alpha: Int
  3145	    ) {
  3146	        val bitmapAspect = bitmap.width.toFloat() / bitmap.height.toFloat()
  3147	        val width: Float
  3148	        val height: Float
  3149	        if (bitmapAspect >= 1f) {
  3150	            width = iconSize
  3151	            height = iconSize / bitmapAspect
  3152	        } else {
  3153	            width = iconSize * bitmapAspect
  3154	            height = iconSize
  3155	        }
  3156	        val top = centerY - height / 2f
  3157	        tempIconRect.set(left, top, left + width, top + height)
  3158	        imagePaint.alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0, 255)
  3159	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempIconRect, imagePaint)
  3160	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  3161	    }

  3163	    private fun drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  3164	        canvas: Canvas,
  3165	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  3166	        top: Float,
  3167	        right: Float,
  3168	        dropdownHeight: Float,
  3169	        stageScale: Float,
  3170	        alpha: Int
  3171	    ) {
  3172	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  3173	        val visibleCount = mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  3174	        val maxOffset = mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown)
  3175	        if (options.isEmpty() || maxOffset <= 0) return
  3176	
  3177	        val trackRight = right - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * stageScale
  3178	        val trackLeft = trackRight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale
  3179	        val trackTop = top + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  3180	        val trackBottom = top + dropdownHeight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  3181	        val trackHeight = (trackBottom - trackTop).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  3182	        val thumbHeight = maxOf(14f * stageScale, trackHeight * visibleCount / options.size)
  3183	            .coerceAtMost(trackHeight)
  3184	        val thumbProgress = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown) / maxOffset.toFloat()
  3185	        val thumbTop = trackTop + (trackHeight - thumbHeight) * thumbProgress
  3186	        val radius = MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  3187	
  3188	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, trackTop, trackRight, trackBottom)
  3189	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.28f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  3190	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  3191	
  3192	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, thumbTop, trackRight, thumbTop + thumbHeight)
  3193	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.78f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  3194	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  3195	    }

  3197	    private fun drawMockSignInField(
  3198	        canvas: Canvas,

  3230	        val floatingBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_TOP_INSET_Y * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  3231	        val labelX = lerpFloat(
  3232	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale,
  3233	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_INSET_X * stageScale,
  3234	            labelProgress
  3235	        )
  3236	        val labelBaselineY = lerpFloat(placeholderBaselineY, floatingBaselineY, labelProgress)
  3237	        canvas.drawText(
  3238	            label,
  3239	            labelX,
  3240	            labelBaselineY,
  3241	            formLabelPaint
  3242	        )
  3243	
  3244	        val fieldText = mockSignInFieldDisplayText(field)
  3245	        val shouldDrawInput = fieldText.isNotEmpty() || focusedMockField == field
  3246	        if (shouldDrawInput) {
  3247	            val inputAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * labelProgress * 255f).roundToInt()
  3248	            formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  3249	            formInputPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  3250	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  3251	            formCaretPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  3252	
  3253	            val inputX = tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale
  3254	            val inputBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_INPUT_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * stageScale
  3255	            val maxInputWidth = fieldWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  3256	            val visibleText = trailingFittingText(fieldText, maxInputWidth, formInputPaint)
  3257	            canvas.drawText(visibleText, inputX, inputBaselineY, formInputPaint)
  3258	
  3259	            if (focusedMockField == field) {
  3260	                val caretX = inputX + formInputPaint.measureText(visibleText) + 2f * stageScale
  3261	                val inputMetrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  3262	                canvas.drawLine(
  3263	                    caretX,
  3264	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.ascent,
  3265	                    caretX,
  3266	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.descent,
  3267	                    formCaretPaint
  3268	                )
  3269	            }
  3270	        }
  3271	    }
  3272	
  3273	    private fun drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  3274	        canvas: Canvas,
  3275	        x: Float,
  3276	        y: Float,
  3277	        stageLeft: Float,
  3278	        stageTop: Float,
  3279	        stageScale: Float,
  3280	        alpha: Float
  3281	    ) {
  3282	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  3283	        val boxLeft = stageLeft + x * stageScale
  3284	        val boxTop = stageTop + y * stageScale
  3285	        val boxRight = boxLeft + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE * stageScale
  3286	        val boxBottom = boxTop + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE * stageScale
  3287	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * if (rememberMeChecked || pressedRememberMe) 46f else 18f).roundToInt()
  3288	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * if (rememberMeChecked) 220f else 168f).roundToInt()
  3289	
  3290	        rememberMeBoxFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  3291	        rememberMeBoxStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(strokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  3292	        rememberMeBoxStrokePaint.strokeWidth = 1.7f * stageScale
  3293	        tempRect.set(boxLeft, boxTop, boxRight, boxBottom)
  3294	        val radius = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_RADIUS * stageScale
  3295	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, rememberMeBoxFillPaint)
  3296	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, rememberMeBoxStrokePaint)
  3297	
  3298	        if (rememberMeChecked) {
  3299	            rememberMeCheckPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  3300	            rememberMeCheckPaint.strokeWidth = 2.45f * stageScale
  3301	            canvas.drawLine(
  3302	                boxLeft + 5.3f * stageScale,
  3303	                boxTop + 11.8f * stageScale,
  3304	                boxLeft + 9.1f * stageScale,
  3305	                boxTop + 15.8f * stageScale,
  3306	                rememberMeCheckPaint
  3307	            )
  3308	            canvas.drawLine(
  3309	                boxLeft + 9.1f * stageScale,
  3310	                boxTop + 15.8f * stageScale,
  3311	                boxLeft + 17.2f * stageScale,
  3312	                boxTop + 6.9f * stageScale,
  3313	                rememberMeCheckPaint
  3314	            )
  3315	            rememberMeCheckPaint.alpha = 255
  3316	        }
  3317	
  3318	        rememberMeTextPaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  3319	        rememberMeTextPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  3320	        rememberMeTextPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  3321	        val metrics = rememberMeTextPaint.fontMetrics
  3322	        val baselineY = (boxTop + boxBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  3323	        canvas.drawText(
  3324	            MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT,
  3325	            boxRight + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_LABEL_GAP * stageScale,
  3326	            baselineY,
  3327	            rememberMeTextPaint
  3328	        )
  3329	        rememberMeTextPaint.alpha = 255
  3330	    }

  3332	    private fun drawBackChevron(
  3333	        canvas: Canvas,
  3334	        centerX: Float,
  3335	        topY: Float,
  3336	        stageLeft: Float,
  3337	        stageTop: Float,
  3338	        stageScale: Float,
  3339	        alpha: Float
  3340	    ) {
  3341	        val chevronAlpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (pressedBackChevron) 0.68f else 0.92f
  3342	        val chevronScale = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  3343	            MOCK_PORTRAIT_STICKY_BOTTOM_SCALE
  3344	        } else {
  3345	            1f
  3346	        }
  3347	        val center = stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  3348	        val top = stageTop + topY * stageScale
  3349	        val bottom = top + MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * chevronScale * stageScale
  3350	        val halfWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_WIDTH * chevronScale * stageScale / 2f
  3351	
  3352	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (chevronAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  3353	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * chevronScale * stageScale
  3354	        canvas.drawLine(center - halfWidth, top, center, bottom, backChevronPaint)
  3355	        canvas.drawLine(center, bottom, center + halfWidth, top, backChevronPaint)
  3356	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  3357	    }

  3359	    private fun decode(resId: Int): Bitmap {
  3360	        return BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, resId, bitmapOptions)
  3361	    }
  3362	
  3363	    private data class CreateAvatarLayers(
  3364	        val bubble: Bitmap,
  3365	        val characters: List<Bitmap>,
  3366	        val bottomStrokes: List<Bitmap>
  3367	    )
  3368	
  3369	    private fun createAvatarCarouselLayers(vararg avatars: Bitmap): CreateAvatarLayers {
  3370	        require(avatars.size == MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT)
  3371	        val bitmapWidth = avatars.first().width
  3372	        val bitmapHeight = avatars.first().height
  3373	        require(avatars.all { it.width == bitmapWidth && it.height == bitmapHeight })
  3374	        return CreateAvatarLayers(
  3375	            bubble = createAvatarBubbleShellBitmap(avatars.first()),
  3376	            characters = avatars.map(::createAvatarCharacterBitmap),
  3377	            bottomStrokes = avatars.map(::createAvatarBottomStrokeBitmap)
  3378	        )
  3379	    }
  3380	
  3381	    private fun createAvatarBottomStrokeBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  3382	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  3383	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  3384	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  3385	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3386	        val strokePixels = IntArray(pixels.size)
  3387	        val minimumBodyY = (bitmapHeight * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BOTTOM_STROKE_START_RATIO).roundToInt()
  3388	
  3389	        for (x in 0 until bitmapWidth) {
  3390	            var bodyBottomY = -1
  3391	            for (y in minimumBodyY until bitmapHeight) {
  3392	                if (isAvatarCharacterFillPixel(pixels[y * bitmapWidth + x])) bodyBottomY = y
  3393	            }
  3394	            if (bodyBottomY < 0) continue
  3395	            val strokeBottomY = minOf(bitmapHeight - 1, bodyBottomY + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BOTTOM_STROKE_DEPTH_PX)
  3396	            for (y in bodyBottomY + 1..strokeBottomY) {
  3397	                val index = y * bitmapWidth + x
  3398	                val sourceAlpha = Color.alpha(pixels[index])
  3399	                if (sourceAlpha <= 16) continue
  3400	                strokePixels[index] = Color.argb(
  3401	                    sourceAlpha,
  3402	                    MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL,
  3403	                    MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL,
  3404	                    MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL
  3405	                )
  3406	            }
  3407	        }
  3408	        val cornerFilledStrokePixels = strokePixels.copyOf()
  3409	        for (y in minimumBodyY until bitmapHeight) {
  3410	            val rowStart = y * bitmapWidth
  3411	            for (x in 0 until bitmapWidth) {
  3412	                if (strokePixels[rowStart + x] == Color.TRANSPARENT) continue
  3413	                val sideStart = maxOf(0, x - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BOTTOM_STROKE_SIDE_EXTEND_PX)
  3414	                val sideEnd = minOf(bitmapWidth - 1, x + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BOTTOM_STROKE_SIDE_EXTEND_PX)
  3415	                for (sideX in sideStart..sideEnd) {
  3416	                    val index = rowStart + sideX
  3417	                    val sourceAlpha = Color.alpha(pixels[index])
  3418	                    if (sourceAlpha <= 16 || isAvatarCharacterFillPixel(pixels[index])) continue
  3419	                    cornerFilledStrokePixels[index] = Color.argb(
  3420	                        sourceAlpha,
  3421	                        MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL,
  3422	                        MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL,
  3423	                        MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL
  3424	                    )
  3425	                }
  3426	            }
  3427	        }
  3428	        return bitmapFromPixels(cornerFilledStrokePixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3429	    }
  3430	
  3431	    private fun isAvatarCharacterFillPixel(pixel: Int): Boolean {
  3432	        if (Color.alpha(pixel) <= 16) return false
  3433	        val channelMax = maxOf(Color.red(pixel), Color.green(pixel), Color.blue(pixel))
  3434	        val channelMin = minOf(Color.red(pixel), Color.green(pixel), Color.blue(pixel))
  3435	        return channelMax > 40 && channelMax - channelMin > 8
  3436	    }
  3437	
  3438	    private fun createAvatarBubbleShellBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  3439	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  3440	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  3441	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  3442	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3443	
  3444	        val centerX = (bitmapWidth - 1f) / 2f
  3445	        val centerY = (bitmapHeight - 1f) / 2f
  3446	        val innerRadius = min(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight) *
  3447	            (0.5f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO)
  3448	        val innerRadiusSquared = innerRadius * innerRadius
  3449	        val shellPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  3450	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  3451	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  3452	            val dx = x - centerX
  3453	            val dy = y - centerY
  3454	            if (dx * dx + dy * dy >= innerRadiusSquared) pixels[index] else Color.TRANSPARENT
  3455	        }
  3456	        return bitmapFromPixels(shellPixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3457	    }
  3458	
  3459	    private fun createAvatarCharacterBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  3460	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  3461	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  3462	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  3463	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3464	
  3465	        val centerX = (bitmapWidth - 1f) / 2f
  3466	        val centerY = (bitmapHeight - 1f) / 2f
  3467	        val innerRadius = min(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight) * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CLIP_RADIUS_RATIO
  3468	        val innerRadiusSquared = innerRadius * innerRadius
  3469	        val distance = IntArray(pixels.size) { -1 }
  3470	        val queue = IntArray(pixels.size)
  3471	        var queueStart = 0
  3472	        var queueEnd = 0
  3473	
  3474	        pixels.indices.forEach { index ->
  3475	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  3476	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  3477	            val dx = x - centerX
  3478	            val dy = y - centerY
  3479	            if (dx * dx + dy * dy < innerRadiusSquared && isAvatarCharacterColor(pixels[index])) {
  3480	                distance[index] = 0
  3481	                queue[queueEnd++] = index
  3482	            }
  3483	        }
  3484	
  3485	        while (queueStart < queueEnd) {
  3486	            val index = queue[queueStart++]
  3487	            val currentDistance = distance[index]
  3488	            if (currentDistance >= MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_OUTLINE_RADIUS_PX) continue
  3489	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  3490	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  3491	            for (dy in -1..1) {
  3492	                val nextY = y + dy
  3493	                if (nextY !in 0 until bitmapHeight) continue
  3494	                for (dx in -1..1) {
  3495	                    if (dx == 0 && dy == 0) continue
  3496	                    val nextX = x + dx
  3497	                    if (nextX !in 0 until bitmapWidth) continue
  3498	                    val nextIndex = nextY * bitmapWidth + nextX
  3499	                    if (distance[nextIndex] >= 0 || !isAvatarOutlinePixel(pixels[nextIndex])) continue
  3500	                    val centerDx = nextX - centerX
  3501	                    val centerDy = nextY - centerY
  3502	                    if (centerDx * centerDx + centerDy * centerDy >= innerRadiusSquared) continue
  3503	                    distance[nextIndex] = currentDistance + 1
  3504	                    queue[queueEnd++] = nextIndex
  3505	                }
  3506	            }
  3507	        }
  3508	
  3509	        val characterPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  3510	            if (distance[index] >= 0) pixels[index] else Color.TRANSPARENT
  3511	        }
  3512	        return bitmapFromPixels(characterPixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3513	    }
  3514	
  3515	    private fun isAvatarCharacterColor(pixel: Int): Boolean {
  3516	        if (Color.alpha(pixel) <= 16) return false
  3517	        val red = Color.red(pixel)
  3518	        val green = Color.green(pixel)
  3519	        val blue = Color.blue(pixel)
  3520	        val channelMax = maxOf(red, green, blue)
  3521	        val channelMin = minOf(red, green, blue)
  3522	        val isNeutralBubbleOrRing = channelMax - channelMin <= 4
  3523	        return (Color.alpha(pixel) >= 250 && !isNeutralBubbleOrRing) ||
  3524	            (channelMax > 40 && channelMax - channelMin > 8)
  3525	    }
  3526	
  3527	    private fun isAvatarOutlinePixel(pixel: Int): Boolean {
  3528	        return Color.alpha(pixel) > 24 && maxOf(Color.red(pixel), Color.green(pixel), Color.blue(pixel)) <= 80
  3529	    }
  3530	
  3531	    private fun bitmapFromPixels(pixels: IntArray, width: Int, height: Int): Bitmap {
  3532	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(width, height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  3533	            setPixels(pixels, 0, width, 0, 0, width, height)
  3534	        }
  3535	    }
  3536	
  3537	    private fun standardSignInBackgroundForSize(): Bitmap? {
  3538	        return when {
  3539	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) &&
  3540	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) -> standardLandscapeBackground
  3541	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) &&
  3542	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) -> standardPortraitBackground
  3543	            else -> null
  3544	        }
  3545	    }
  3546	
  3547	    private fun isSizeCloseTo(actual: Int, expected: Int): Boolean {
  3548	        return abs(actual - expected) <= STANDARD_TABLET_SIZE_TOLERANCE
  3549	    }
  3550	
  3551	    private fun isFinalFrameSettled(): Boolean {
  3552	        return IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis > 0L &&
  3553	            bootProgressAt(IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis) >= 1f
  3554	    }
  3555	
  3556	    private fun settledAvatarHitProfile(x: Float, y: Float): CinerificProfile? {
  3557	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) return null
  3558	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return null
  3559	
  3560	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  3561	
  3562	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  3563	            val avatarSize = portraitAvatarSize()
  3564	            val centerX = stage.left + FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  3565	            val halfWidth = (avatarSize / 2f + PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_HIT_HORIZONTAL_PADDING) * stage.scale
  3566	            val top = stage.top + portraitAvatarTop() * stage.scale
  3567	            val bottom = stage.top + (
  3568	                portraitProfileNameTop() + portraitProfileNameHeight() +
  3569	                    PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_HIT_BOTTOM_PADDING
  3570	                ) * stage.scale
  3571	            return if (x in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) && y in top..bottom) {
  3572	                FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[portraitProfileIndex].profile
  3573	            } else {
  3574	                null
  3575	            }
  3576	        }
  3577	
  3578	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  3579	        return FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.firstOrNull { target ->
  3580	            val bounds = target.bounds
  3581	            val centerX = stage.left + (bounds.x + bounds.w / 2f) * stage.scale
  3582	            val centerY = stage.top + (bounds.y + stackShiftY + bounds.h / 2f) * stage.scale
  3583	            val radius = min(bounds.w, bounds.h) * stage.scale / 2f
  3584	            val dx = x - centerX
  3585	            val dy = y - centerY
  3586	            dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius
  3587	        }?.profile
  3588	    }

  3590	    private fun settledCreateAccountPromptHit(x: Float, y: Float): Boolean {
  3591	        if (!accountPromptCanOpen(MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount)) return false
  3592	
  3593	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return false
  3594	        if (portraitIntroAmount() >= 0.999f) {
  3595	            return portraitPromptHit(
  3596	                x,
  3597	                y,
  3598	                portraitPromptCreateCenterY(),
  3599	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_HIT_WIDTH
  3600	            )
  3601	        }
  3602	        val centerX = stage.left + accountPromptCreateCenterX() * stage.scale
  3603	        val centerY = accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale) +
  3604	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY()) * stage.scale
  3605	        val halfWidth = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_HIT_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  3606	        val halfHeight = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_HIT_HEIGHT * stage.scale / 2f
  3607	        return x in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) &&
  3608	            y in (centerY - halfHeight)..(centerY + halfHeight)
  3609	    }

  3653	    private fun portraitPromptHit(x: Float, y: Float, centerY: Float, hitWidth: Float): Boolean {
  3654	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return false
  3655	        val physicalCenterX = stage.left + FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  3656	        val physicalCenterY = stage.top + centerY * stage.scale
  3657	        val halfWidth = hitWidth * PORTRAIT_ACTION_SCALE * stage.scale / 2f
  3658	        val halfHeight = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_HIT_HEIGHT * PORTRAIT_ACTION_SCALE * stage.scale / 2f
  3659	        return x in (physicalCenterX - halfWidth)..(physicalCenterX + halfWidth) &&
  3660	            y in (physicalCenterY - halfHeight)..(physicalCenterY + halfHeight)
  3661	    }

  3663	    private fun accountPromptCanOpen(flow: MockAccountFlow): Boolean {
  3664	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled() || isMockSignInClosing() || isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) return false
  3665	        if (usesPortraitStackedAuthLayout()) return false
  3666	        return mockSignInStartMillis == null || activeMockFlow != flow
  3667	    }
  3668	
  3669	    private fun accountPromptStageTop(stageScale: Float): Float {
  3670	        return (height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * stageScale) / 2f
  3671	    }
  3672	
  3673	    private fun mockSignInFieldHit(x: Float, y: Float): MockSignInField? {
  3674	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null) return null
  3675	
  3676	        val baseStage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  3677	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  3678	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  3679	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  3680	        if (activeMockFlow in listOf(
  3681	                MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount,
  3682	                MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  3683	            ) &&
  3684	            !portraitAuthPointerInViewport(y, baseStage)
  3685	        ) return null

  3697	            val fieldX = mockActiveFieldX(field)

  3817	    private fun updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3818	        if (!activeMockCreateAvatarDrag) return false
  3819	
  3820	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  3821	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockTouchDownY
  3822	        if (
  3823	            !mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  3824	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > touchSlop &&
  3825	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > abs(dragDeltaY)
  3826	        ) {
  3827	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = true
  3828	            lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = if (mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX < 0f) {
  3829	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next
  3830	            } else {
  3831	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous
  3832	            }
  3833	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  3834	        }
  3835	
  3836	        if (mockCreateAvatarDragMoved) {
  3837	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3838	        }
  3839	        return true
  3840	    }

  3842	    private fun mockDropdownOptionHit(pointerX: Float, pointerY: Float): MockDropdownOption? {
  3843	        if (
  3844	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  3845	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount ||
  3846	            expandedMockDropdown == null
  3847	        ) {
  3848	            return null
  3849	        }
  3850	
  3851	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return null
  3852	        val baseStage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  3853	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  3854	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  3855	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  3856	        if (!portraitAuthPointerInViewport(pointerY, baseStage)) return null
  3857	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3858	        val dropdownX = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  3859	        val yStart = mockDropdownOptionsY(dropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3860	        val left = stage.left + dropdownX * stage.scale
  3861	        val right = stage.left + (dropdownX + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stage.scale
  3862	        if (pointerX !in left..right) return null
  3863	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  3864	            val scrollbarLeft = right -
  3865	                (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stage.scale
  3866	            if (pointerX >= scrollbarLeft) return null
  3867	        }
  3868	
  3869	        mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown).forEachIndexed { optionIndex, option ->
  3870	            val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown)
  3871	            val top = stage.top + (yStart + optionIndex * optionHeight) * stage.scale
  3872	            val bottom = top + optionHeight * stage.scale
  3873	            if (pointerY in top..bottom) return MockDropdownOption(dropdown, option)
  3874	        }
  3875	        return null
  3876	    }

  3893	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)

  3898	        val top = stage.top + yStart * stage.scale

  3903	    private fun updateMockDropdownScroll(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3904	        val dropdown = activeMockDropdownScroll ?: return false
  3905	        if (expandedMockDropdown != dropdown || !mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) return false
  3906	
  3907	        val baseStage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return true
  3908	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  3909	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  3910	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  3911	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3912	        val optionHeightPx = (mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  3913	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockDropdownScrollStartY
  3914	        if (abs(dragDeltaY) > touchSlop) {
  3915	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = true
  3916	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  3917	        }
  3918	
  3919	        if (mockDropdownScrollMoved) {
  3920	            val rowDelta = ((mockDropdownScrollStartY - event.y) / optionHeightPx).roundToInt()
  3921	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, mockDropdownScrollStartOffset + rowDelta)
  3922	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3923	        }
  3924	        return true
  3925	    }

  3927	    private fun updatePortraitAuthScroll(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3928	        if (
  3929	            !isPortraitIntroLayout() ||
  3930	            activeMockFlow !in listOf(
  3931	                MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount,
  3932	                MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  3933	            ) ||
  3934	            activeMockDropdownScroll != null
  3935	        ) {
  3936	            return false
  3937	        }
  3938	
  3939	        val deltaX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  3940	        val deltaY = event.y - portraitAuthScrollStartY
  3941	        if (!portraitAuthScrollMoved) {
  3942	            if (abs(deltaY) <= touchSlop || abs(deltaY) <= abs(deltaX)) return false
  3943	            portraitAuthScrollMoved = true
  3944	            pressedMockField = null
  3945	            pressedMockDropdown = null
  3946	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  3947	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  3948	            activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  3949	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  3950	            expandedMockDropdown = null
  3951	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  3952	        }
  3953	
  3954	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return true
  3955	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  3956	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  3957	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  3958	        val maxScrollY = when (activeMockFlow) {
  3959	            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword -> portraitForgotPasswordMaxScrollY(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3960	            else -> portraitCreateMaxScrollY(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3961	        }
  3962	        portraitAuthScrollY = (portraitAuthScrollStartOffset - deltaY).coerceIn(0f, maxScrollY)
  3963	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3964	        return true
  3965	    }

  3981	    private fun shouldStartMockDragReturn(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3982	        if (mockDragReturnInProgress || isMockSignInClosing()) return false
  3983	
  3984	        val upwardDragY = mockTouchDownY - event.y
  3985	        if (upwardDragY <= mockBackDragThresholdPx()) return false
  3986	
  3987	        val dragX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  3988	        return upwardDragY > abs(dragX)
  3989	    }
  3990	
  3991	    private fun mockBackDragThresholdPx(): Float {
  3992	        val stageThreshold = currentStageMetrics()?.let { stage ->
  3993	            MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD * stage.scale
  3994	        } ?: MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD
  3995	        return maxOf(stageThreshold, touchSlop * 3f)
  3996	    }

  4073	    private fun mockBackChevronContains(
  4074	        pointerX: Float,
  4075	        pointerY: Float,
  4076	        centerX: Float,
  4077	        topY: Float,
  4078	        stage: StageMetrics
  4079	    ): Boolean {
  4080	        val chevronScale = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4081	            MOCK_PORTRAIT_STICKY_BOTTOM_SCALE
  4082	        } else {
  4083	            1f
  4084	        }
  4085	        val center = stage.left + centerX * stage.scale
  4086	        val hitHalfWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HIT_WIDTH * chevronScale * stage.scale / 2f
  4087	        val left = center - hitHalfWidth
  4088	        val right = center + hitHalfWidth
  4089	        val backStageTop = if (usesPortraitStackedAuthLayout()) {
  4090	            stage.top
  4091	        } else {
  4092	            accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale)
  4093	        }
  4094	        val top = backStageTop +
  4095	            (topY - (MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HIT_HEIGHT - MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HEIGHT) *
  4096	                chevronScale / 2f) * stage.scale
  4097	        val bottom = top + MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HIT_HEIGHT * chevronScale * stage.scale
  4098	        return pointerX in left..right && pointerY in top..bottom
  4099	    }

  4101	    private fun mockCreateAvatarBounds(columnX: Float, firstFieldY: Float): Bounds {
  4102	        val scaledAvatarSize = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE
  4103	        return Bounds(
  4104	            columnX + (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH - scaledAvatarSize) / 2f,
  4105	            firstFieldY +
  4106	                MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LABEL_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE +
  4107	                MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_TOP_GAP * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE,
  4108	            scaledAvatarSize,
  4109	            scaledAvatarSize
  4110	        )
  4111	    }
  4112	
  4113	    private fun mockCreateAvatarCarouselBounds(
  4114	        columnX: Float,
  4115	        firstFieldY: Float,
  4116	        avatarBounds: Bounds
  4117	    ): Bounds {
  4118	        val centerX = columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f
  4119	        val contentTop = firstFieldY
  4120	        val contentBottom = avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h
  4121	        val contentCenterY = (contentTop + contentBottom) / 2f
  4122	        val chevronSpan = avatarBounds.w +
  4123	            (MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_SIDE_GAP + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_WIDTH / 2f) * 2f
  4124	        val side = maxOf(chevronSpan, contentBottom - contentTop)
  4125	        return Bounds(
  4126	            centerX - side / 2f,
  4127	            contentCenterY - side / 2f,
  4128	            side,
  4129	            side
  4130	        )
  4131	    }
  4132	
  4133	    private fun mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(scaledAvatarSize: Float): Float {
  4134	        return scaledAvatarSize + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_SIDE_GAP * 2f
  4135	    }
  4136	
  4137	    private fun mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(
  4138	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  4139	        avatarBounds: Bounds
  4140	    ): Float {
  4141	        return if (direction == MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous) {
  4142	            avatarBounds.x - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_SIDE_GAP
  4143	        } else {
  4144	            avatarBounds.x + avatarBounds.w + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_SIDE_GAP
  4145	        }
  4146	    }
  4147	
  4148	    private fun mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds: Bounds): Float {
  4149	        return avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h / 2f
  4150	    }
  4151	
  4152	    private fun mockCreateAvatarChevronContains(
  4153	        pointerX: Float,
  4154	        pointerY: Float,
  4155	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  4156	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  4157	        stage: StageMetrics
  4158	    ): Boolean {
  4159	        val centerX = stage.left + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * stage.scale
  4160	        val centerY = stage.top + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stage.scale
  4161	        val halfWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  4162	        val halfHeight = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_HEIGHT * stage.scale / 2f
  4163	        return pointerX in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) &&
  4164	            pointerY in (centerY - halfHeight)..(centerY + halfHeight)
  4165	    }
  4166	
  4167	    private fun focusMockSignInField(field: MockSignInField) {
  4168	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = field
  4169	        mockInputDimActiveField = field
  4170	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
  4171	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  4172	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  4173	        if (focusedMockField != field) {
  4174	            activeComposingText = ""
  4175	            focusedMockField = field
  4176	            restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  4177	            restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  4178	        }
  4179	        requestFocus()
  4180	        post {
  4181	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  4182	            inputMethodManager?.restartInput(this)
  4183	            inputMethodManager?.showSoftInput(this, InputMethodManager.SHOW_IMPLICIT)
  4184	        }
  4185	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  4186	    }
  4187	
  4188	    private fun clearMockSignInFieldFocus() {
  4189	        if (focusedMockField == null) return
  4190	        activeComposingText = ""
  4191	        focusedMockField = null
  4192	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
  4193	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  4194	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  4195	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  4196	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  4197	        val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  4198	        inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  4199	        clearFocus()
  4200	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  4201	    }

  4267	    private fun mockSignInContentStageMetrics(
  4268	        stage: StageMetrics,
  4269	        yOffset: Float,
  4270	        focusShiftY: Float
  4271	    ): StageMetrics {
  4272	        if (!usesPortraitStackedAuthLayout(MockAccountFlow.SignIn) ||
  4273	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn
  4274	        ) return stage
  4275	
  4276	        val contentScale = stage.scale * portraitCreateContentScale()
  4277	        val centerX = stage.left + FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  4278	        val firstFieldY = mockPrimaryFormY() + yOffset + focusShiftY
  4279	        val firstFieldTop = stage.top + firstFieldY * stage.scale
  4280	        return StageMetrics(
  4281	            left = centerX - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * contentScale / 2f,
  4282	            top = firstFieldTop - firstFieldY * contentScale,
  4283	            scale = contentScale
  4284	        )
  4285	    }

  4288	        stage: StageMetrics,
  4289	        yOffset: Float,

  4297	        val formTop = stage.top + formTopY * stage.scale - portraitAuthScrollY
  4298	        return StageMetrics(
  4299	            left = centerX - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * contentScale / 2f,
  4300	            top = formTop - formTopY * contentScale,
  4301	            scale = contentScale
  4302	        )
  4303	    }

  4305	    private fun portraitAuthViewportTopPx(stage: StageMetrics): Float {
  4306	        val title = when (activeMockFlow) {
  4307	            MockAccountFlow.SignIn -> ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT
  4308	            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount -> ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT
  4309	            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword -> ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT
  4310	            null -> null
  4311	        }
  4312	        if (title != null) {
  4313	            val titleTextSize = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * PORTRAIT_ACTION_SCALE * stage.scale
  4314	            val titleBaseline = mockFormTitleTargetBaseline(
  4315	                title,
  4316	                mockPrimaryFormY(),
  4317	                stage.top,
  4318	                stage.scale,
  4319	                titleTextSize
  4320	            )
  4321	            formTitlePaint.textSize = titleTextSize
  4322	            return titleBaseline + formTitlePaint.fontMetrics.descent +
  4323	                mockFormFieldGap() * stage.scale * portraitCreateContentScale()
  4324	        }
  4325	        return stage.top +
  4326	            (portraitLogoVisibleBottom() + MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_VIEWPORT_GAP) * stage.scale
  4327	    }
  4328	
  4329	    private fun portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage: StageMetrics): Float {
  4330	        return height - systemBarInsetBottomPx -
  4331	            MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_VIEWPORT_BOTTOM_RESERVE *
  4332	            MOCK_PORTRAIT_STICKY_BOTTOM_SCALE * stage.scale
  4333	    }

  4337	            y in portraitAuthViewportTopPx(stage)..portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)

  4340	    private fun mockCreateContentStageMetrics(
  4341	        stage: StageMetrics,
  4342	        yOffset: Float,
  4343	        focusShiftY: Float
  4344	    ): StageMetrics {
  4345	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return stage
  4346	
  4347	        val contentScale = stage.scale * portraitCreateContentScale()
  4348	        val centerX = stage.left + FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  4349	        val firstFieldY = mockPrimaryFormY() + yOffset + focusShiftY
  4350	        val firstFieldTop = stage.top + firstFieldY * stage.scale - portraitAuthScrollY
  4351	        return StageMetrics(
  4352	            left = centerX - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * contentScale / 2f,
  4353	            top = firstFieldTop - firstFieldY * contentScale,
  4354	            scale = contentScale
  4355	        )
  4356	    }

  4358	    private fun portraitCreateMaxScrollY(
  4359	        stage: StageMetrics,
  4360	        yOffset: Float,
  4361	        focusShiftY: Float
  4362	    ): Float {
  4363	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return 0f
  4364	        val contentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4365	        val avatarSectionY = mockCreateAvatarSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4366	        val avatarBottomY = mockCreateAvatarBounds(
  4367	            mockCreateAvatarColumnX(),
  4368	            avatarSectionY
  4369	        ).let { it.y + it.h }
  4370	        val finalControlBottomY = mockDropdownY(
  4371	            MockDropdown.Year,
  4372	            yOffset,
  4373	            focusShiftY
  4374	        ) + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT
  4375	        val contentBottomY = maxOf(avatarBottomY, finalControlBottomY)
  4376	        val contentBottom = contentStage.top + contentBottomY * contentStage.scale +
  4377	            MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_CONTENT_BOTTOM_GAP * stage.scale + portraitAuthScrollY
  4378	        return (contentBottom - portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  4379	    }

  4381	    private fun portraitForgotPasswordMaxScrollY(
  4382	        stage: StageMetrics,
  4383	        yOffset: Float,
  4384	        focusShiftY: Float
  4385	    ): Float {
  4386	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return 0f
  4387	        val contentStage = mockForgotPasswordContentStageMetrics(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4388	        val helpBottomY = mockForgotPasswordHelpBottomY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4389	        val contentBottom = contentStage.top + helpBottomY * contentStage.scale +
  4390	            MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_CONTENT_BOTTOM_GAP * stage.scale + portraitAuthScrollY
  4391	        return (contentBottom - portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  4392	    }

  4468	    private fun mockForgotPasswordBodyBottomY(formTopY: Float): Float {
  4469	        val paragraphs = mockForgotPasswordBodyText().split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n"))
  4470	        val copyWidth = mockForgotPasswordCopyWidth()
  4471	        var bottomY = mockParagraphBottomY(
  4472	            paragraphs.firstOrNull().orEmpty(),
  4473	            formTopY + mockForgotPasswordBodyTopGap(),
  4474	            copyWidth,
  4475	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_TEXT_SIZE,
  4476	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_LINE_HEIGHT
  4477	        )
  4478	        paragraphs.getOrNull(1)?.let { middleText ->
  4479	            bottomY = mockParagraphBottomY(
  4480	                middleText,
  4481	                bottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  4482	                copyWidth,
  4483	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  4484	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT
  4485	            )
  4486	        }
  4487	        val bottomText = paragraphs.drop(2).joinToString("\n\n")
  4488	        if (bottomText.isNotEmpty()) {
  4489	            bottomY = mockParagraphBottomY(
  4490	                bottomText,
  4491	                bottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  4492	                copyWidth,
  4493	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  4494	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT
  4495	            )
  4496	        }
  4497	        return bottomY
  4498	    }

  4531	    private fun mockForgotRecoverySelectorLayout(
  4532	        left: Float,
  4533	        width: Float,
  4534	        labelWidth: Float,
  4535	        boxWidth: Float
  4536	    ): ForgotRecoverySelectorLayout {
  4537	        val gap = ((width - labelWidth - boxWidth * 2f) / 4f).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  4538	        val labelX = left + gap
  4539	        val usernameBoxX = labelX + labelWidth + gap
  4540	        val passwordBoxX = usernameBoxX + boxWidth + gap
  4541	        return ForgotRecoverySelectorLayout(
  4542	            labelX = labelX,
  4543	            usernameBoxX = usernameBoxX,
  4544	            passwordBoxX = passwordBoxX,
  4545	            boxWidth = boxWidth
  4546	        )
  4547	    }
