  4549	    private fun mockForgotRecoverySelectorBoxWidth(

  4553	    ): Float {

  4555	        val currentHorizontalPadding = ((baseBoxWidth - widestTextWidth) / 2f).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  4556	        return widestTextWidth + currentHorizontalPadding * 4f
  4557	    }

  4567	    private fun mockActiveFieldX(field: MockSignInField): Float {
  4568	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) return mockForgotPasswordFormX()
  4569	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return mockFormX()
  4570	
  4571	        val firstColumnX = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) mockCreatePortraitColumnX() else mockCreateFormX()
  4572	        val middleColumnX = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4573	            mockCreatePortraitColumnX()
  4574	        } else {
  4575	            mockCreateFormX() + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH + MOCK_CREATE_FORM_COLUMN_GAP
  4576	        }
  4577	        return when (field) {
  4578	            MockSignInField.Username,
  4579	            MockSignInField.Password,
  4580	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> firstColumnX
  4581	            MockSignInField.Email -> middleColumnX
  4582	            MockSignInField.Month -> middleColumnX
  4583	            MockSignInField.Day -> middleColumnX + MOCK_DATE_MONTH_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_FIELD_GAP
  4584	            MockSignInField.Year -> middleColumnX + MOCK_DATE_MONTH_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DAY_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_FIELD_GAP * 2f
  4585	        }
  4586	    }

  4588	    private fun mockActiveFieldY(field: MockSignInField, yOffset: Float, focusShiftY: Float): Float {
  4589	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  4590	            return mockForgotPasswordFieldY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4591	        }
  4592	
  4593	        val usesSecondSection = activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  4594	            isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  4595	            field in listOf(
  4596	                MockSignInField.Email,
  4597	                MockSignInField.Month,
  4598	                MockSignInField.Day,
  4599	                MockSignInField.Year
  4600	            )
  4601	        val sectionTop = if (usesSecondSection) {
  4602	            mockCreateSecondSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4603	        } else {
  4604	            mockPrimaryFormY() + yOffset + focusShiftY
  4605	        }
  4606	        val fieldOffsetY = if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  4607	            when (field) {
  4608	                MockSignInField.Username -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4609	                    mockCreatePortraitUsernameOffsetY()
  4610	                } else {
  4611	                    0f
  4612	                }
  4613	                MockSignInField.Email -> 0f
  4614	                MockSignInField.Password -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4615	                    mockCreatePortraitPasswordOffsetY()
  4616	                } else {
  4617	                    MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + mockFormFieldGap() * 2f
  4618	                }
  4619	                MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4620	                    mockCreatePortraitPasswordOffsetY() +
  4621	                        MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + mockFormFieldGap()
  4622	                } else {
  4623	                    MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT * 2f + mockFormFieldGap() * 3f
  4624	                }
  4625	                MockSignInField.Month,
  4626	                MockSignInField.Day,
  4627	                MockSignInField.Year -> mockCreateDateRowOffsetY()
  4628	            }
  4629	        } else {
  4630	            activeMockFields().indexOf(field).coerceAtLeast(0) *
  4631	                (MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + mockFormFieldGap())
  4632	        }
  4633	        return sectionTop + fieldOffsetY
  4634	    }

  4647	    private fun mockActiveFormCenterX(): Float {

  4781	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  4782	        val optionCount = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size
  4783	        val viewportCount = if (mockDropdownUsesDateViewport(dropdown)) {
  4784	            MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_VISIBLE_OPTION_COUNT
  4785	        } else {
  4786	            optionCount
  4787	        }
  4788	        return min(optionCount, viewportCount)
  4789	    }
  4790	
  4791	    private fun mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown: MockDropdown): Boolean {
  4792	        return mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size > mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  4793	    }
  4794	
  4795	    private fun mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  4796	        return (mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] ?: 0).coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  4797	    }
  4798	
  4799	    private fun mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  4800	        return (mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size - mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)).coerceAtLeast(0)
  4801	    }
  4802	
  4803	    private fun setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown, offset: Int) {
  4804	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = offset.coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  4805	    }
  4806	
  4807	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown: MockDropdown): List<String> {
  4808	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  4809	        val offset = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown)
  4810	        return options.drop(offset).take(mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown))
  4811	    }
  4812	
  4813	    private fun prepareMockDropdownScroll(dropdown: MockDropdown) {
  4814	        if (!mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  4815	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, 0)
  4816	            return
  4817	        }
  4818	
  4819	        val selectedIndex = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).indexOf(mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown))
  4820	        setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, if (selectedIndex >= 0) selectedIndex else 0)
  4821	    }

  4890	    private fun setMockForgotRecoveryTarget(target: MockForgotRecoveryTarget) {

  4902	        if (focusedMockField != null) {

  4904	        } else {
  4905	            restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  4906	            restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  4907	            restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  4908	            restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  4909	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  4910	        }

  4945	    private fun startMockForgotPasswordSubmission() {

  4949	        }

  4951	        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  4952	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading
  4953	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  4954	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  4955	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  4956	    }
  4957	
  4958	    private fun mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress(): Float {
  4959	        val startMillis = forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis ?: return if (
  4960	            forgotPasswordSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  4961	        ) {
  4962	            1f
  4963	        } else {
  4964	            0f
  4965	        }
  4966	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  4967	        return (elapsed / MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SUBMIT_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4968	    }

  4972	    }

  4974	    private fun finishMockForgotPasswordSubmissionIfNeeded() {
  4975	        val startMillis = forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis ?: return
  4976	        if (forgotPasswordSubmissionState != MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading) {
  4977	            forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null
  4978	            return
  4979	        }
  4980	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SUBMIT_MS) return
  4981	
  4982	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  4983	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null
  4984	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  4985	    }

  4990	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null
  4991	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  4992	    }

  4994	    private fun startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(
  4995	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  4996	        startProgress: Float = 0f
  4997	    ) {
  4998	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount || isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()) return
  4999	
  5000	        val nextIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(direction)
  5001	        if (nextIndex == mockCreateAvatarIndex) return
  5002	
  5003	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  5004	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
  5005	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex = nextIndex
  5006	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection = direction
  5007	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartProgress = startProgress.coerceIn(0f, 0.96f)
  5008	        mockCreateAvatarIndex = nextIndex
  5009	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  5010	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  5011	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  5012	    }

  5035	    private fun portraitProfileSwipeDirection(): MockAvatarCarouselDirection? {

  5037	            portraitProfileDragDeltaX < 0f -> MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next
  5038	            portraitProfileDragDeltaX > 0f -> MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous
  5039	            else -> null
  5040	        }

  5043	    private fun portraitProfileDragProgress(): Float {
  5044	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return 0f
  5045	        val slideDistancePx = (portraitAvatarSize() * stage.scale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  5046	        return (abs(portraitProfileDragDeltaX) / slideDistancePx).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5047	    }

  5049	    private fun portraitProfileCarouselProgress(): Float {
  5050	        val startMillis = portraitProfileCarouselStartMillis ?: return 1f
  5051	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  5052	        val elapsedProgress = (elapsed / MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CAROUSEL_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5053	        return lerpFloat(portraitProfileCarouselStartProgress, 1f, elapsedProgress).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5054	    }

  5065	        portraitProfileCarouselFromIndex = portraitProfileIndex

  5092	    private fun mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(): Float {
  5093	        val baseStage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return 0f
  5094	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  5095	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  5096	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  5097	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  5098	        val scaledAvatarSize = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE
  5099	        return mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(scaledAvatarSize), stage.scale)
  5100	    }
  5101	
  5102	    private fun mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(slideDistance: Float, stageScale: Float): Float {
  5103	        val slideDistancePx = (slideDistance * stageScale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  5104	        return (abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) / slideDistancePx).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5105	    }
  5106	
  5107	    private fun mockCreateAvatarCarouselProgress(): Float {
  5108	        val startMillis = mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis ?: return 1f
  5109	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  5110	        val elapsedProgress = (elapsed / MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CAROUSEL_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5111	        return lerpFloat(mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartProgress, 1f, elapsedProgress).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5112	    }

  5115	        return mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis != null
  5116	    }

  5246	    private fun isSoftKeyboardVisible(): Boolean {
  5247	        val rootInsets = ViewCompat.getRootWindowInsets(this)
  5248	        if (rootInsets != null) {
  5249	            val imeInsets = rootInsets.getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime())
  5250	            if (rootInsets.isVisible(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime()) || imeInsets.bottom > 0) return true
  5251	        }
  5252	
  5253	        val rootHeight = rootView?.height ?: 0
  5254	        if (rootHeight <= 0) return false
  5255	        getWindowVisibleDisplayFrame(tempWindowRect)
  5256	        val obscuredHeight = rootHeight - tempWindowRect.height()
  5257	        return obscuredHeight > rootHeight * 0.18f
  5258	    }

  5350	    private fun setMockSignInFieldText(field: MockSignInField, text: String) {

  5354	            MockSignInField.Password -> passwordText = limitedText
  5355	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> confirmPasswordText = limitedText
  5356	            MockSignInField.Email -> emailText = limitedText
  5357	            MockSignInField.Month -> monthText = limitedText
  5358	            MockSignInField.Day -> dayText = limitedText
  5359	            MockSignInField.Year -> yearText = limitedText
  5360	        }
  5361	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword && field == MockSignInField.Email) {
  5362	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  5363	        }
  5364	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  5365	        if (mockInputDimAwaitingField == field) {
  5366	            mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
  5367	        }
  5368	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  5369	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  5370	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  5371	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  5372	    }

  5374	    private fun appendMockSignInInput(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  5375	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  5376	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  5377	        if (input.isEmpty()) return true
  5378	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  5379	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  5380	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  5381	        val insertedText = input.take(availableChars)
  5382	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + insertedText)
  5383	        activeComposingText = ""
  5384	        return true
  5385	    }
  5386	
  5387	    private fun replaceMockSignInComposingText(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  5388	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  5389	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  5390	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  5391	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  5392	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  5393	        val composingText = input.take(availableChars)
  5394	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + composingText)
  5395	        activeComposingText = composingText
  5396	        return true
  5397	    }
  5398	
  5399	    private fun finishMockSignInComposingText(): Boolean {
  5400	        activeComposingText = ""
  5401	        return true
  5402	    }
  5403	
  5404	    private fun deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength: Int): Boolean {
  5405	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  5406	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  5407	        if (currentText.isEmpty()) {
  5408	            activeComposingText = ""
  5409	            return true
  5410	        }
  5411	        val deleteCount = beforeLength.coerceAtLeast(1).coerceAtMost(currentText.length)
  5412	        activeComposingText = ""
  5413	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, currentText.dropLast(deleteCount))
  5414	        return true
  5415	    }

  5417	    private fun handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  5418	        activeComposingText = ""
  5419	        val fields = activeMockFields()
  5420	        val fieldIndex = fields.indexOf(focusedMockField)
  5421	        if (fieldIndex >= 0 && fieldIndex < fields.lastIndex && actionCode != EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE) {
  5422	            focusMockSignInField(fields[fieldIndex + 1])
  5423	        } else if (
  5424	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  5425	            focusedMockField == MockSignInField.Email &&
  5426	            mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  5427	        ) {
  5428	            startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  5429	        } else {
  5430	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  5431	            inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  5432	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  5433	        }
  5434	        return true
  5435	    }
  5436	
  5437	    private fun handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  5438	        if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  5439	        return when (keyCode) {
  5440	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  5441	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  5442	            else -> {
  5443	                val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  5444	                typedCharacter?.let(::appendMockSignInInput) ?: false
  5445	            }
  5446	        }
  5447	    }
  5448	
  5449	    private fun sanitizeMockSignInInput(input: String): String {
  5450	        return buildString {
  5451	            input.forEach { character ->
  5452	                if (character != '\n' && character != '\r' && !character.isISOControl()) {
  5453	                    append(character)
  5454	                }
  5455	            }
  5456	        }
  5457	    }
  5458	
  5459	    private fun trailingFittingText(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): String {
  5460	        if (paint.measureText(text) <= maxWidth) return text
  5461	
  5462	        var startIndex = 0
  5463	        while (startIndex < text.length && paint.measureText(text.substring(startIndex)) > maxWidth) {
  5464	            startIndex += 1
  5465	        }
  5466	        return text.substring(startIndex)
  5467	    }
  5468	
  5469	    private fun wrappedTextLines(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): List<String> {
  5470	        val lines = mutableListOf<String>()
  5471	        text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n")).forEachIndexed { paragraphIndex, paragraph ->
  5472	            if (paragraphIndex > 0) {
  5473	                lines += ""
  5474	            }
  5475	
  5476	            var currentLine = ""
  5477	            paragraph.replace('\n', ' ')
  5478	                .split(Regex("\\s+"))
  5479	                .filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
  5480	                .forEach { word ->
  5481	                    val candidateLine = if (currentLine.isEmpty()) word else "$currentLine $word"
  5482	                    if (currentLine.isEmpty() || paint.measureText(candidateLine) <= maxWidth) {
  5483	                        currentLine = candidateLine
  5484	                    } else {
  5485	                        lines += currentLine
  5486	                        currentLine = word
  5487	                    }
  5488	                }
  5489	            if (currentLine.isNotEmpty()) {
  5490	                lines += currentLine
  5491	            }
  5492	        }
  5493	        return lines
  5494	    }
  5495	
  5496	    private fun moveToward(current: Float, target: Float, amount: Float): Float {
  5497	        return when {
  5498	            current < target -> (current + amount).coerceAtMost(target)
  5499	            current > target -> (current - amount).coerceAtLeast(target)
  5500	            else -> current
  5501	        }
  5502	    }
  5503	
  5504	    private fun currentStageMetrics(): StageMetrics? {
  5505	        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return null
  5506	
  5507	        val standardScale = min(width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  5508	        val portraitScale = portraitDesignScale()
  5509	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  5510	        val stageScale = lerpFloat(standardScale, portraitScale, portraitAmount)
  5511	        val standardTop = (height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * standardScale) / 2f
  5512	        return StageMetrics(
  5513	            left = (width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f,
  5514	            top = lerpFloat(standardTop, 0f, portraitAmount) +
  5515	                mockAuthOpenStageLiftY(stageScale) +
  5516	                mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale),
  5517	            scale = stageScale
  5518	        )
  5519	    }

  5523	    private fun portraitIntroAmount(): Float {
  5524	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return 0f
  5525	        // Keep the portrait welcome composition fixed while an auth panel slides in.
  5526	        return 1f
  5527	    }

  5529	    private fun portraitDesignScale(): Float {
  5530	        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return 1f
  5531	
  5532	        val widthScale = width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH
  5533	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return widthScale
  5534	
  5535	        val referenceDesignHeight = FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * PORTRAIT_REFERENCE_HEIGHT_TO_WIDTH
  5536	        return min(widthScale, height / referenceDesignHeight)
  5537	    }

  5634	    }

  5636	    private fun portraitPromptForgotCenterY(): Float {
  5637	        return portraitPromptCreateCenterY() +
  5638	            portraitPromptCenterSpacing() * PORTRAIT_LINK_SPACING_SCALE * 2f
  5639	    }
  5640	
  5641	    private fun portraitContentOffsetY(): Float {
  5642	        val logoHeight = LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE
  5643	        val rawLogoTop = portraitStackCenter(1f / 6f) - logoHeight / 2f
  5644	        val rawForgotCenter = portraitStackCenter(5f / 6f) + portraitPromptCenterSpacing()
  5645	        val topBreathingRoom = rawLogoTop + PORTRAIT_LOGO_WHITE_EYE_TOP_INSET - portraitSafeTop()
  5646	        val bottomBreathingRoom = portraitSafeBottom() -
  5647	            (rawForgotCenter + PORTRAIT_PROMPT_VISIBLE_HALF_HEIGHT)
  5648	        return (bottomBreathingRoom - topBreathingRoom) / 2f
  5649	    }
  5650	
  5651	    private fun portraitOuterBreathingRoom(): Float {
  5652	        val logoHeight = LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE
  5653	        val rawLogoTop = portraitStackCenter(1f / 6f) - logoHeight / 2f
  5654	        val rawForgotCenter = portraitStackCenter(5f / 6f) + portraitPromptCenterSpacing()
  5655	        val rawTopRoom = rawLogoTop + PORTRAIT_LOGO_WHITE_EYE_TOP_INSET - portraitSafeTop()
  5656	        val rawBottomRoom = portraitSafeBottom() -
  5657	            (rawForgotCenter + PORTRAIT_PROMPT_VISIBLE_HALF_HEIGHT)
  5658	        return (rawTopRoom + rawBottomRoom) / 2f
  5659	    }
  5660	
  5661	    private fun portraitVerticalRoomProgress(): Float {
  5662	        val usableHeight = portraitSafeBottom() - portraitSafeTop()
  5663	        return ((usableHeight - PORTRAIT_COMPACT_DESIGN_HEIGHT) /
  5664	            (PORTRAIT_COMFORTABLE_DESIGN_HEIGHT - PORTRAIT_COMPACT_DESIGN_HEIGHT))
  5665	            .coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5666	    }
  5667

  5669	        return lerpFloat(
  5670	            PORTRAIT_COMPACT_PROMPT_CENTER_SPACING,
  5671	            PORTRAIT_COMFORTABLE_PROMPT_CENTER_SPACING,
  5672	            portraitVerticalRoomProgress()
  5673	        )
  5674	    }

  5679	        return MOCK_AUTH_OPEN_LANDSCAPE_STAGE_LIFT_Y * stageScale * liftMotion
  5680	    }

  5707	    private fun currentIntroSnapshot(): CinerificIntroSnapshot {

  5709	            ?.takeIf { mockSignInStartMillis != null && mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress > 0f }
  5710	            ?.name
  5711	            .orEmpty()

  5718	            monthText = monthText,
  5719	            dayText = dayText,
  5720	            yearText = yearText,
  5721	            subscriptionTierText = normalizedMembershipTierSelection(subscriptionTierText),
  5722	            forgotRecoveryTargetName = forgotRecoveryTarget.name,
  5723	            forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName = forgotPasswordSubmissionState.name,

  5725	            createAvatarIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  5726	            portraitProfileIndex = portraitProfileIndex
  5727	        )

  5730	    private fun notifyIntroSnapshotChanged() {
  5731	        val snapshot = currentIntroSnapshot()
  5732	        if (appliedIntroSnapshot == snapshot) return
  5733	
  5734	        appliedIntroSnapshot = snapshot
  5735	        onIntroSnapshotChanged?.invoke(snapshot)
  5736	    }

  5738	    private fun switchMockAccountFlow(flow: MockAccountFlow) {
  5739	        if (
  5740	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  5741	            activeMockFlow == flow ||
  5742	            isMockSignInClosing() ||
  5743	            isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()
  5744	        ) {
  5745	            return
  5746	        }
  5747	
  5748	        val previousFlow = activeMockFlow ?: return
  5749	        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  5750	        activeComposingText = ""
  5751	        expandedMockDropdown = null
  5752	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  5753	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  5754	        portraitAuthScrollY = 0f
  5755	        portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
  5756	        pressedMockField = null
  5757	        pressedRememberMe = false
  5758	        pressedBackChevron = false
  5759	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  5760	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  5761	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  5762	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  5763	        lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
  5764	        pressedMockDropdown = null
  5765	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  5766	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  5767	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  5768	        resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()
  5769	        if (flow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  5770	            forgotRecoveryTarget = MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password
  5771	            emailText = ""
  5772	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  5773	        }
  5774	        outgoingMockFlow = previousFlow
  5775	        activeMockFlow = flow
  5776	        mockFlowSwitchStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  5777	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  5778	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  5779	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  5780	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  5781	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  5782	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  5783	    }
  5784	
  5785	    private fun openMockSignInScreen() {
  5786	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null && mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 1f) return
  5787	        val currentProgress = mockSignInProgress()
  5788	        activeMockFlow = MockAccountFlow.SignIn
  5789	        mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  5790	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = currentProgress
  5791	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
  5792	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  5793	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  5794	    }

  5800	        portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
  5801	        activeMockFlow = MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
  5802	        mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  5803	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = currentProgress
  5804	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f

  5830	        pressedBackChevron = false
  5831	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  5832	        pressedMockDropdown = null
  5833	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  5834	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  5835	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  5836	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  5837	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  5838	        portraitAuthScrollY = 0f
  5839	        portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
  5840	        activeComposingText = ""
  5841	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
  5842	        usernameText = ""
  5843	        passwordText = ""
  5844	        confirmPasswordText = ""
  5845	        emailText = ""
  5846	        monthText = MOCK_FORM_MONTH_TEXT
  5847	        dayText = MOCK_FORM_DAY_TEXT
  5848	        yearText = MOCK_FORM_YEAR_TEXT
  5849	        subscriptionTierText = MOCK_FORM_SUBSCRIPTION_TIER_TEXT
  5850	        forgotRecoveryTarget = MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password
  5851	        resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  5852	        resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()

  5857	            mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = 0
  5858	        }
  5859	        focusedMockField = null
  5860	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  5861	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  5862	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  5863	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  5864	        val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  5865	        inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  5866	        clearFocus()
  5867	        mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  5868	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = currentProgress
  5869	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
  5870	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  5871	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  5872	    }
  5873	
  5874	    private fun mockSignInProgress(): Float {
  5875	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return 0f
  5876	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  5877	        val progress = (elapsed / mockSignInTransitionDurationMs().toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5878	        return lerpFloat(
  5879	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress,
  5880	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress,
  5881	            progress
  5882	        )
  5883	    }
  5884	
  5885	    private fun mockFlowSwitchProgress(): Float {
  5886	        val startMillis = mockFlowSwitchStartMillis ?: return 1f
  5887	        return ((SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis) / MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS.toFloat())
  5888	            .coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5889	    }
  5890	
  5891	    private fun isMockFlowSwitchAnimating(): Boolean {
  5892	        return mockFlowSwitchStartMillis != null && mockFlowSwitchProgress() < 1f
  5893	    }
  5894	
  5895	    private fun finishMockFlowSwitchIfNeeded() {
  5896	        if (mockFlowSwitchStartMillis == null || mockFlowSwitchProgress() < 1f) return
  5897	        outgoingMockFlow = null
  5898	        mockFlowSwitchStartMillis = null
  5899	    }
  5900	
  5901	    private fun isMockSignInAnimating(): Boolean {
  5902	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return false
  5903	        return SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()
  5904	    }
  5905	
  5906	    private fun isMockSignInClosing(): Boolean {
  5907	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f
  5908	    }
  5909	
  5910	    private fun mockSignInTransitionDurationMs(): Long {
  5911	        val distance = abs(mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress - mockSignInTransitionStartProgress)
  5912	        return (MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS * distance)
  5913	            .roundToInt()
  5914	            .coerceAtLeast(1)
  5915	            .toLong()
  5916	    }
  5917	
  5918	    private fun finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded() {
  5919	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return
  5920	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()) return
  5921	
  5922	        if (mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f) {
  5923	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
  5924	            activeMockFlow = null
  5925	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
  5926	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
  5927	            pressedMockField = null
  5928	            pressedRememberMe = false
  5929	            pressedBackChevron = false
  5930	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  5931	            pressedMockDropdown = null
  5932	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  5933	            pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  5934	            pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  5935	            activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  5936	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  5937	            mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  5938	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  5939	            resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()
  5940	            notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  5941	            return
  5942	        }
  5943	
  5944	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
  5945	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
  5946	    }
  5947	
  5948	    private inner class MockSignInInputConnection : BaseInputConnection(this@CinerificIntroView, false) {
  5949	        override fun commitText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {
  5950	            return appendMockSignInInput(text?.toString().orEmpty())
  5951	        }
  5952	
  5953	        override fun setComposingText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {
  5954	            return replaceMockSignInComposingText(text?.toString().orEmpty())
  5955	        }
  5956	
  5957	        override fun finishComposingText(): Boolean {
  5958	            return finishMockSignInComposingText()
  5959	        }
  5960	
  5961	        override fun deleteSurroundingText(beforeLength: Int, afterLength: Int): Boolean {
  5962	            return deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength)
  5963	        }
  5964	
  5965	        override fun deleteSurroundingTextInCodePoints(beforeLength: Int, afterLength: Int): Boolean {
  5966	            return deleteSurroundingText(beforeLength, afterLength)
  5967	        }
  5968	
  5969	        override fun getTextBeforeCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
  5970	            val text = focusedMockField?.let(::mockSignInFieldText).orEmpty()
  5971	            return text.takeLast(n.coerceAtLeast(0))
  5972	        }
  5973	
  5974	        override fun getTextAfterCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
  5975	            return ""
  5976	        }
  5977	
  5978	        override fun getSelectedText(flags: Int): CharSequence {
  5979	            return ""
  5980	        }
  5981	
  5982	        override fun performEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  5983	            return handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode)
  5984	        }
  5985	
  5986	        override fun sendKeyEvent(event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  5987	            if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  5988	            return when (event.keyCode) {
  5989	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  5990	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  5991	                else -> {
  5992	                    val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  5993	                    if (typedCharacter != null) {
  5994	                        appendMockSignInInput(typedCharacter)
  5995	                    } else {
  5996	                        super.sendKeyEvent(event)
  5997	                    }
  5998	                }
  5999	            }
  6000	        }
  6001	    }
  6002	}

  6158	private inline fun <reified T : Enum<T>> enumValueOrNull(name: String): T? {
  6159	    return runCatching { enumValueOf<T>(name) }.getOrNull()
  6160	}
  6161	
  6162	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  6163	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  6164	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  6165	}
  6166	
  6167	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  6168	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  6169	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  6170	}
  6171	
  6172	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  6173	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  6174	}
  6175	
  6176	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  6177	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  6178	}
  6179	
  6180	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  6181	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  6182	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  6183	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  6184	    return base + bounce
  6185	}
  6186	
  6187	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  6188	
  6189	private fun lerpBounds(start: Bounds, end: Bounds, amount: Float) = Bounds(
  6190	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  6191	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  6192	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  6193	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  6194	)
  6195	
  6196	private fun offsetBounds(bounds: Bounds, yOffset: Float) = Bounds(
  6197	    x = bounds.x,
  6198	    y = bounds.y + yOffset,
  6199	    w = bounds.w,
  6200	    h = bounds.h
  6201	)
  6202	
  6203	private fun lerpColor(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  6204	    val t = amount.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  6205	    return Color.argb(
  6206	        lerpInt(Color.alpha(start), Color.alpha(end), t),
  6207	        lerpInt(Color.red(start), Color.red(end), t),
  6208	        lerpInt(Color.green(start), Color.green(end), t),
  6209	        lerpInt(Color.blue(start), Color.blue(end), t)
  6210	    )
  6211	}
  6212	
  6213	private fun lerpInt(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  6214	    return (start + (end - start) * amount).roundToInt()
  6215	}
  6216	
  6217	private fun combineBitmaps(base: Bitmap, overlay: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  6218	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(base.width, base.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  6219	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  6220	    val paint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  6221	    canvas.drawBitmap(base, 0f, 0f, paint)
  6222	    canvas.drawBitmap(overlay, 0f, 0f, paint)
  6223	    return output
  6224	}
  6225	
  6226	private fun circularBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  6227	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(source.width, source.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  6228	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  6229	    val rect = RectF(0f, 0f, source.width.toFloat(), source.height.toFloat())
  6230	    val maskPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)
  6231	    val imagePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  6232	    canvas.drawOval(rect, maskPaint)
  6233	    imagePaint.xfermode = PorterDuffXfermode(PorterDuff.Mode.SRC_IN)
  6234	    canvas.drawBitmap(source, null, rect, imagePaint)
  6235	    imagePaint.xfermode = null
  6236	    return output
  6237	}

============================================================
FILE: README.md
============================================================

     1	# Cinerific
     2	
     3	Local Android project for the `prahlin/cinerific` GitHub repo.
     4	
     5	This is a first Kotlin / Jetpack Compose scaffold based on the Figma intro and sign-in frames. The current app opens as a tablet-friendly full-screen frame preview with:
     6	
     7	- Intro frame 1
     8	- Animated intro frame 2
     9	- Promo/logo intro frame 3
    10	- Sign-in/loading frame 4
    11	
    12	## Stack
    13	
    14	- Kotlin
    15	- Jetpack Compose Material 3
    16	- Android Gradle Plugin 8.5.x
    17	- Gradle 8.7
    18	
    19	## Run it
    20	
    21	Open the project in Android Studio and run `app`, or use the Gradle wrapper:
    22	
    23	```bash
    24	./gradlew :app:assembleDebug
    25	```
    26	
    27	The project pins Gradle to Android Studio's bundled JBR in `gradle.properties`, so command-line builds do not accidentally use an unsupported system JDK.
    28	
    29	From VS Code, run the default build task `Android: assemble debug`. There are also tasks for `Android: install debug` and `Android: launch Cinerific` when an emulator or device is connected.
    30	
    31	### Standard tablet emulator
    32	
    33	Use the guarded launcher for the persistent low-load QA profile:
    34	
    35	```bash
    36	./start_cinerific_android_standard.sh
    37	```
    38	
    39	The launcher requests and verifies `1280x800` at `160 dpi`, two guest CPU cores,
    40	host GPU acceleration, and the repository-owned rounded tablet bezel before
    41	opening Cinerific. It refuses to continue if the running AVD does not match.
    42	Emulator logs are written to
    43	`~/Library/Logs/Cinerific/standard-emulator.log`.
    44	
    45	## Notes for the first Kotlin pass
    46	
    47	- The layout is intentionally simple and adaptive for tablets.
    48	- The current visuals are Compose approximations of the Figma frames and are meant to be replaced with production assets screen by screen.
    49	- The local project folder is the same path as the GitHub remote: `prahlin/cinerific`.

============================================================
FILE: start_cinerific_android_standard.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	set -Eeuo pipefail
     3	
     4	ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
     5	SDK="${ANDROID_HOME:-${ANDROID_SDK_ROOT:-$HOME/Library/Android/sdk}}"
     6	EMU="${SDK}/emulator/emulator"
     7	ADB="${SDK}/platform-tools/adb"
     8	AVD="Cinerific_Standard_Tablet"
     9	AVD_CONFIG="$HOME/.android/avd/${AVD}.avd/config.ini"
    10	DEVICE="emulator-5554"
    11	PORT="5554"
    12	APP_PACKAGE="com.prahlin.cinerific"
    13	APP_ACTIVITY="${APP_PACKAGE}/.MainActivity"
    14	EXPECTED_SIZE="1280x800"
    15	EXPECTED_DENSITY="160"
    16	EXPECTED_CORES="2"
    17	EXPECTED_SKIN="cinerific_pixel_tablet_1280"
    18	BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS="${BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS:-180}"
    19	LOG_DIR="$HOME/Library/Logs/Cinerific"
    20	LOG="$LOG_DIR/standard-emulator.log"
    21	ERROR_LOG="$LOG_DIR/standard-emulator.err.log"
    22	LAUNCHD_LABEL="com.cinerific.standardavd"
    23	LAUNCHD_PLIST="$ROOT/launchd/${LAUNCHD_LABEL}.plist"
    24	
    25	fail() {
    26	  printf 'ERROR: %s\n' "$*" >&2
    27	  exit 1
    28	}
    29	
    30	[[ -x "$EMU" ]] || fail "Android emulator not found at $EMU"
    31	[[ -x "$ADB" ]] || fail "adb not found at $ADB"
    32	[[ -f "$LAUNCHD_PLIST" ]] || fail "LaunchAgent not found at $LAUNCHD_PLIST"
    33	[[ -f "$AVD_CONFIG" ]] || fail "AVD configuration not found at $AVD_CONFIG"
    34	"$EMU" -list-avds | grep -qx "$AVD" || fail "AVD $AVD was not found"
    35	
    36	config_skin="$(sed -n 's/^skin.name=//p' "$AVD_CONFIG")"
    37	config_frame="$(sed -n 's/^showDeviceFrame=//p' "$AVD_CONFIG")"
    38	[[ "$config_skin" == "$EXPECTED_SKIN" ]] || fail "Expected skin $EXPECTED_SKIN, got $config_skin"
    39	[[ "$config_frame" == "yes" ]] || fail "Device frame is not enabled in $AVD_CONFIG"
    40	
    41	mkdir -p "$LOG_DIR"
    42	
    43	device_is_connected() {
    44	  "$ADB" devices | awk -v device="$DEVICE" \
    45	    '$1 == device && $2 == "device" { found = 1 } END { exit !found }'
    46	}
    47	
    48	if device_is_connected; then
    49	  actual_avd="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" emu avd name 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r' | sed -n '1p')"
    50	  [[ "$actual_avd" == "$AVD" ]] || fail "$DEVICE is running $actual_avd, not $AVD"
    51	  printf '%s is already running.\n' "$AVD"
    52	else
    53	  printf 'Starting %s with the low-load QA profile...\n' "$AVD"
    54	  launchctl bootout "gui/$(id -u)/${LAUNCHD_LABEL}" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
    55	  : > "$LOG"
    56	  : > "$ERROR_LOG"
    57	  launchctl bootstrap "gui/$(id -u)" "$LAUNCHD_PLIST"
    58	fi
    59	
    60	deadline=$((SECONDS + BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS))
    61	until device_is_connected; do
    62	  (( SECONDS < deadline )) || fail "Emulator did not connect within ${BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS}s; see $LOG"
    63	  sleep 2
    64	done
    65	
    66	until [[ "$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell getprop sys.boot_completed 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r')" == "1" ]]; do
    67	  (( SECONDS < deadline )) || fail "Android did not finish booting within ${BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS}s; see $LOG"
    68	  sleep 2
    69	done
    70	
    71	actual_avd="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" emu avd name 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r' | sed -n '1p')"
    72	actual_size="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell wm size | sed -n 's/^Physical size: //p' | tr -d '\r')"
    73	actual_density="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell wm density | sed -n 's/^Physical density: //p' | tr -d '\r')"
    74	actual_cores="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell 'grep -c "^processor" /proc/cpuinfo' | tr -d '\r')"
    75	
    76	[[ "$actual_avd" == "$AVD" ]] || fail "Expected $AVD, got $actual_avd"
    77	[[ "$actual_size" == "$EXPECTED_SIZE" ]] || fail "Expected $EXPECTED_SIZE, got $actual_size"
    78	[[ "$actual_density" == "$EXPECTED_DENSITY" ]] || fail "Expected density $EXPECTED_DENSITY, got $actual_density"
    79	[[ "$actual_cores" == "$EXPECTED_CORES" ]] || fail "Expected $EXPECTED_CORES cores, got $actual_cores"
    80	
    81	sleep 10
    82	device_is_connected || fail "Emulator disconnected after boot; see $LOG"
    83	
    84	if "$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell pm path "$APP_PACKAGE" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    85	  "$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell am force-stop "$APP_PACKAGE"
    86	  "$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell am start -n "$APP_ACTIVITY" >/dev/null
    87	  printf 'Cinerific launched on %s.\n' "$DEVICE"
    88	else
    89	  printf 'Cinerific is not installed on %s; emulator verification still passed.\n' "$DEVICE"
    90	fi
    91	
    92	printf 'Verified: %s @ %s dpi, %s cores, host GPU.\n' \
    93	  "$actual_size" "$actual_density" "$actual_cores"
    94	printf 'Emulator log: %s\n' "$LOG"


============================================================
FILE: read_project.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	
     3	PROJECT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
     4	OUTPUT_FILE="$PROJECT_DIR/cinerific_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt"
     5	
     6	echo "Generating Cinerific project snapshot..."
     7	echo "Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR"
     8	echo "Snapshot: $OUTPUT_FILE"
     9	echo ""
    10	
    11	write_header() {
    12	  cat > "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
    13	Cinerific Android PROJECT SNAPSHOT
    14	Generated: $(date)
    15	Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR
    16	
    17	IMPORTANT INSTRUCTIONS FOR CHATGPT & CODEX:
    18	1. Read this entire snapshot before giving code edits.
    19	2. Preserve all assets, spacing, alignment, fonts, images, layout, animations, and relative positioning unless specifically asked to change them.
    20	3. Do not remove unrelated code.
    21	4. Do not rewrite large sections unless requested.
    22	5. When giving code changes, include exact line/row numbers whenever possible.
    23	6. Print full scripts when requested.
    24	7. Treat this project structure as the source of truth.
    25	8. If you don't understand something, ask for clarification instead of making assumptions.
    26	9. Warn me, or at least caution me, if I ask for something that would break the project or cause it to not work as intended.
    27	
    28	
    29	============================================================
    30	SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
    31	============================================================
    32	
    33	This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
    34	Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.
    35	
    36	The snapshot intentionally ignores:
    37	- .git
    38	- .gradle
    39	- .idea
    40	- app/build
    41	- build
    42	- .kotlin
    43	- .tmp
    44	- .cache
    45	- .DS_Store
    46	- local.properties
    47	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
    48	- *.jks
    49	- *.keystore
    50	
    51	Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.
    52	
    53	
    54	============================================================
    55	PROJECT FILE TREE
    56	============================================================
    57	
    58	EOF
    59	}
    60	
    61	print_file() {
    62	  local file="$1"
    63	
    64	  if [ -f "$file" ]; then
    65	    {
    66	      echo ""
    67	      echo ""
    68	      echo "============================================================"
    69	      echo "FILE: ${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}"
    70	      echo "============================================================"
    71	      echo ""
    72	      nl -ba "$file"
    73	    } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    74	  fi
    75	}
    76	
    77	print_binary_inventory() {
    78	  local title="$1"
    79	  local dir="$2"
    80	
    81	  {
    82	    echo ""
    83	    echo ""
    84	    echo "============================================================"
    85	    echo "$title"
    86	    echo "============================================================"
    87	    echo ""
    88	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    89	
    90	  if [ ! -d "$dir" ]; then
    91	    echo "Directory not present: ${dir#$PROJECT_DIR/}" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    92	    return
    93	  fi
    94	
    95	  find "$dir" -type f \
    96	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
    97	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
    98	    ! -name "*.jks" \
    99	    ! -name "*.keystore" \
   100	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   101	    local size
   102	    local modified
   103	    size="$(stat -f "%z" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%s" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   104	    modified="$(stat -f "%Sm" -t "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%y" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   105	    printf '%s | %s bytes | modified %s\n' "${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}" "$size" "$modified" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   106	  done
   107	}
   108	
   109	write_snapshot() {
   110	  write_header
   111	
   112	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" \
   113	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git" -prune -o \
   114	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.gradle" -prune -o \
   115	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.idea" -prune -o \
   116	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build" -prune -o \
   117	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/build" -prune -o \
   118	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.kotlin" -prune -o \
   119	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.tmp" -prune -o \
   120	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.cache" -prune -o \
   121	    -name ".DS_Store" -prune -o \
   122	    -name "local.properties" -prune -o \
   123	    -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" -prune -o \
   124	    -name "*.jks" -prune -o \
   125	    -name "*.keystore" -prune -o \
   126	    -print | sort >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   127	
   128	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   129	
   130	
   131	============================================================
   132	FILE CONTENTS — ANDROID SOURCE
   133	============================================================
   134	
   135	EOF
   136	
   137	  for file in \
   138	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml" \
   139	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/MainActivity.kt" \
   140	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificApp.kt" \
   141	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificIntroView.kt" \
   142	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/theme/Theme.kt" \
   143	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml" \
   144	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values/themes.xml" \
   145	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values-night/themes.xml" \
   146	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values-v31/themes.xml" \
   147	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/splash_transparent.xml" \
   148	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher_background.xml" \
   149	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher_foreground.xml" \
   150	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher.xml" \
   151	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher_round.xml"
   152	  do
   153	    print_file "$file"
   154	  done
   155	
   156	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   157	
   158	
   159	============================================================
   160	FILE CONTENTS — ROOT / TOOLING
   161	============================================================
   162	
   163	EOF
   164	
   165	  for file in \
   166	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.gitignore" \
   167	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.github/copilot-instructions.md" \
   168	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.vscode/mcp.json" \
   169	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.vscode/tasks.json" \
   170	    "$PROJECT_DIR/README.md" \
   171	    "$PROJECT_DIR/build.gradle.kts" \
   172	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build.gradle.kts" \
   173	    "$PROJECT_DIR/settings.gradle.kts" \
   174	    "$PROJECT_DIR/gradle.properties" \
   175	    "$PROJECT_DIR/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.properties" \
   176	    "$PROJECT_DIR/launchd/com.cinerific.standardavd.plist" \
   177	    "$PROJECT_DIR/start_cinerific_android_standard.sh" \
   178	    "$PROJECT_DIR/read_project.sh"
   179	  do
   180	    print_file "$file"
   181	  done
   182	
   183	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / IMAGE ASSET INVENTORY — DRAWABLE-NODPI" "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable-nodpi"
   184	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / IMAGE ASSET INVENTORY — EMULATOR SKINS" "$PROJECT_DIR/emulator/skins"
   185	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / SCREENSHOT INVENTORY" "$PROJECT_DIR/screenshots"
   186	
   187	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   188	
   189	
   190	============================================================
   191	NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
   192	============================================================
   193	
   194	The Cinerific snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private directories:
   195	- .git
   196	- .gradle
   197	- .idea
   198	- app/build
   199	- build
   200	- .kotlin
   201	- .tmp
   202	- .cache
   203	- .DS_Store
   204	- local.properties
   205	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
   206	- *.jks
   207	- *.keystore
   208	
   209	The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
   210	
   211	EOF
   212	}
   213	
   214	write_snapshot
   215	
   216	echo "Done."
   217	echo ""
   218	echo "Snapshot created here:"
   219	echo "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   220	echo ""
   221	echo "Open it with:"
   222	echo "open \"$OUTPUT_FILE\""

============================================================
NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
============================================================

The Cinerific snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private directories:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.jks
- *.keystore

The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
